# Console diagnostics. Run in the country named by each prefix unless the effect
# scopes explicitly. Resets only close wars whose package-owned flag is present.
ic_dak_doa_test_start = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = FUL country_exists = FRE }
		NLF = { nlf_dak_doa_begin = yes }
		log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|START|credits_before=[?FRE.FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience_before=[?FRE.FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]"
	}
}

ic_dak_doa_test_choose_hold = { if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { fre_dak_doa_commit_hold = yes } } }
ic_dak_doa_test_choose_gm100 = { if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { fre_dak_doa_commit_gm100 = yes } } }
ic_dak_doa_test_choose_evacuation = { if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { fre_dak_doa_commit_evacuation = yes } } }

ic_dak_doa_test_advance_to_hold_boundary = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active } }
		NLF = {
			set_province_controller = 1328
			set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 6 }
			log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|BOUNDARY|hold=6|required=7|elapsed=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]"
		}
	}
}

ic_dak_doa_test_reset_streak = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = FUL }
		FUL = { set_province_controller = 1328 }
		NLF = { nlf_dak_doa_update_hold = yes }
	}
}

ic_dak_doa_test_force_clean = { if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 1 } nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes } } }
ic_dak_doa_test_force_costly = { if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 2 } nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes } } }
ic_dak_doa_test_force_failure = { if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 4 } nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes } } }
ic_dak_doa_test_force_superseded = { if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 5 } nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes } } }

ic_dak_doa_test_inspect = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		NLF = {
			set_temp_variable = { ic_dak_war_ful = 0 }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FUL has_war_with = FUL } set_temp_variable = { ic_dak_war_ful = 1 } }
			log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|INSPECT|active=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days|0]|outcome=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome|0]|war_ful=[?ic_dak_war_ful|0]"
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { set_temp_variable = { response_validation_package = 7 } response_package_validation_log = yes }
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE country_exists = NLF VIE = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|WIDER_WAR|VIE_NLF=preserved" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = NLF FRE = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|WIDER_WAR|FRE_NLF=preserved" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA country_exists = NLF FRA = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "IC_TEST|DAK_DOA|WIDER_WAR|FRA_NLF=preserved" }
}

ic_dak_doa_test_reset = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		NLF = {
			nlf_dak_doa_finish = yes
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Clean
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Costly
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Aborted
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Failure
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded
			set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed = 0 }
			set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 0 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			fre_dak_doa_response_cleanup = yes
			fre_dak_doa_response_clear_results = yes
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_GM100_Survived
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_GM100_Mauled
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Isolated_Garrison_Lost
		}
	}
}

ic_northwest_recovery_test_record_original_failure = {
	VIN = {
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Failure
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Tay_Bac
	}
}

ic_northwest_recovery_test_start = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { VIN_northwest_recovery_target_available_trigger = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Attempted } }
			log = "IC_TEST|NW_RECOVERY|START|supply_before=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]|pp_before=[?political_power|0]"
			set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Attempted
			set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Active
			add_political_power = -50
			set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = -75 }
			vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
			set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_id = 9 }
			vin_campaign_begin = yes
			vin_campaign_declare_war = yes
		}
	}
}

ic_northwest_recovery_test_advance_to_hold_boundary = {
	VIN = {
		set_province_controller = 10075
		set_province_controller = 12075
		set_province_controller = 13775
		set_province_controller = 16526
		set_province_controller = 16527
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 4 }
		log = "IC_TEST|NW_RECOVERY|BOUNDARY|hold=4|required=5|elapsed=[?VIN_camp_days_elapsed|0]"
	}
}

ic_northwest_recovery_test_force_failure = { VIN = { set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 4 } vin_campaign_resolve = yes } }
ic_northwest_recovery_test_force_costly_success = { VIN = { set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 2 } vin_campaign_resolve = yes } }
ic_northwest_recovery_test_force_superseded = { VIN = { set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 5 } vin_campaign_resolve = yes } }

ic_northwest_recovery_test_inspect = {
	VIN = {
		set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_owns_gate = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_fre_war = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_tai_war = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_cleanup_complete = 0 }
		if = { limit = { owns_state = 1761 } set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_owns_gate = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE has_war_with = FRE } set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_fre_war = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI } set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_tai_war = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Cleanup_Complete } set_temp_variable = { ic_nw_cleanup_complete = 1 } }
		log = "IC_TEST|NW_RECOVERY|INSPECT|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|days=[?VIN_camp_days_elapsed|0]|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]|outcome=[?VIN_camp_last_outcome|0]|owns_1761=[?ic_nw_owns_gate|0]|war_FRE=[?ic_nw_fre_war|0]|war_TAI=[?ic_nw_tai_war|0]|cleanup_complete=[?ic_nw_cleanup_complete|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]|pp=[?political_power|0]"
	}
}

ic_northwest_recovery_test_reset = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending
			vin_northwest_recovery_close_owned_war = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Cleanup_Complete
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War
		vin_northwest_recovery_clear_war_snapshot = yes
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Attempted
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Northwest_Recovery
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Result_Costly
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Result_Aborted
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Result_Failure
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Result_Superseded
		remove_mission = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Objective
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 0 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } } set_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 0 } }
	}
}

# Shared response selectors used by checkpoint branches for accepted packages.
# Their lifecycle remains owned by the original package effects.
ic_response_test_resource_snapshot = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { log = "IC_TEST|RESOURCE|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|manpower_k=[?manpower_k|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { log = "IC_TEST|RESOURCE|campaign_supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]|pp=[?political_power|0]" } }
}

# ============================================================================
# CEFEO (FRE) QA / testing effects
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Run from the console with:   e <effect_name>
# Most tests assume you are playing FRE (`tag FRE` first); the war-score tests
# work from any tag because the struggle scores live on FRA.
#
# The struggle-core and Geneva test sections live in this file too. This section covers the CEFEO
# rework: Metropole Patience, the FRA lifeline, GCMA/VNA bonuses, the
# operation gates, Operation Castor, the Dien Bien Phu arc, command rotation
# and dissolution.
#
# Quick reference:
#   -- status / patience --
#   e test_fre_status            log patience, tier, commander bonus, GCMA, VNA
#   e test_indochina_command_status  verify the CEFEO faction, both commands, crown-domain custody, and separate wars
#   e test_indochina_command_repair  rebuild the early command model after a test/save migration
#   e test_fre_patience_set_0    patience to 0 (fires the milestone chain)
#   e test_fre_patience_set_50   patience to 50
#   e test_fre_patience_set_100  patience to 100
#   -- lifeline / allies --
#   e test_fre_request_all       fire all five FRA request events at FRA
#   e test_fre_gcma_max          GCMA network to 4 (fires Trinquier dilemma)
#   e test_fre_roll_battle_prep  log a battle-prep success roll with bonuses
#   -- operations / battles --
#   e test_fre_ops_unlock        waive date gates, clear cooldowns + activations
#   e test_fre_credits_grant     +500 War Credits so every tier is affordable
#   e test_fre_operation_launch  open the generic commitment prompt (Brochet by default)
#   e test_fre_operation_resolve fire the watchdog as if the 90-day timer expired
#   e test_fre_operation_threshold  force the deterministic outcome
#   e test_fre_operation_finish  clear all operation state, fire no outcome
#   e test_fre_operation_status  log current op, tier, states and credits
#   e test_fre_castor_start_full start the exact-province full Castor deployment
#   e test_fre_castor_advance    move an active Castor to its next daily check
#   e test_fre_castor_day_14     exercise the exact objective-completion edge
#   e test_fre_castor_fail       force an active Castor failure
#   e test_fre_castor_supersede  force an active Castor supersession
#   e test_fre_castor_status     log Castor lifecycle and hold counters
#   e test_fre_castor_reset      clear Castor test state and its camp formation
#   e test_fre_pollux_start_historical  start the historical split evacuation
#   e test_fre_pollux_start_escort      start the protected Route Pavie withdrawal
#   e test_fre_pollux_day_7      exercise the escorted/airlift completion edge
#   e test_fre_pollux_fail       force the active evacuation's column-loss result
#   e test_fre_pollux_supersede  force the theatre-superseded result
#   e test_fre_pollux_status     log posture, route, counters, and exclusive result
#   e test_fre_pollux_reset      clear Pollux test state and rebuild no formation
#   e test_fre_atlante_start_full       start the full central-Vietnam commitment
#   e test_fre_atlante_start_vietnamese start the Vietnamese-led coastal plan
#   e test_fre_atlante_choose_north     cancel Atlante and bank the northern reserve
#   e test_fre_atlante_day_14           exercise the selected objective's completion edge
#   e test_fre_atlante_fail             force the active offensive's stalled result
#   e test_fre_atlante_supersede        force the theatre-superseded result
#   e test_fre_atlante_status           log posture, objectives, counters, and result exclusivity
#   e test_fre_atlante_reset            clear Atlante test state
#   e test_fre_lorraine_status   log Lorraine posture and exclusive result
#   e test_fre_hoa_binh_status   log phase-two posture and exclusive result
#   e test_fre_hoa_binh_day_105  force a planned Amarante request at day 105
#   e test_fre_command_next      advance the command rotation one step
#   -- Tai highlands / Dien Bien Phu ownership --
#   e test_tai_status            log who owns 1761 and 671 and whether TAI is alive
#   e test_vin_tay_bac_win       fire the Northwest win at VIN (rump TAI must survive at 671)
#   e test_dbp_tai_collapse      run the camp-falls collapse (TAI annexed by VIN)
#   e test_vin_dbp_fallback      fire the no-Castor dispatcher at VIN
#   -- endings / dissolution --
#   e test_fre_win_war           push FRA scores past the victory trigger
#   e test_fre_lose_war          push FRA scores past the defeat trigger
#   e test_fre_winddown_now      start the wind-down chain immediately
#   e test_fre_dissolve_vie      dissolve the corps into VIE now
#   e test_fre_dissolve_vin      dissolve the corps into VIN now
# ============================================================================

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Status / patience
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

test_fre_status = {
	log = "[FRE TEST] patience=[?FRE.FRE_Metropole_Patience|0] cmd_bonus=[?FRE.FRE_Commander_Mission_Bonus|0] gcma=[?FRE.FRE_GCMA_Network|0] vna=[?FRE.FRE_VNA_Coordination|0]"
	log = "[FRE TEST] profrance=[?FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore|0] anticomm=[?FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore|0] comm=[?FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore|0]"
}

test_fre_patience_set_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = -200 }
			fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_patience_set_50 = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_variable = { FRE_Metropole_Patience = 50 }
			fre_update_metropole_tier = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_patience_set_100 = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = 200 }
			fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
		}
	}
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lifeline / allies
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

test_fre_request_all = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = {
			country_event = { id = FRE_Metropole.1 days = 1 }
			country_event = { id = FRE_Metropole.2 days = 2 }
			country_event = { id = FRE_Metropole.3 days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = FRE_Metropole.4 days = 4 }
			country_event = { id = FRE_Metropole.5 days = 5 }
		}
	}
}

test_fre_gcma_max = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			fre_gcma_expand_network = yes
			fre_gcma_expand_network = yes
			fre_gcma_expand_network = yes
			fre_gcma_expand_network = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_roll_battle_prep = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			fre_mission_calculate_battle_prep_success = yes
			log = "[FRE TEST] battle_prep success chance = [?mission_success_chance]"
		}
	}
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Operations / battles
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

test_fre_ops_unlock = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_country_flag = FRE_Operations_Accelerated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Lorraine
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Hirondelle
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Mouette
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Brochet
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Bretagne
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Adolphe
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Camargue
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Castor
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Pollux
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Atlante
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Lorraine_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Hirondelle_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Mouette_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Brochet_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Bretagne_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Adolphe_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Camargue_Activated
			fre_castor_force_superseded_cleanup = yes
			fre_pollux_force_superseded_cleanup = yes
			fre_atlante_force_superseded_cleanup = yes
		}
	}
}

# Advance the command rotation: Carpentier -> de Lattre -> Salan -> Navarre.
test_fre_command_next = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = FRE_Command_De_Lattre }
				country_event = { id = FRE_Command.4 days = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_idea = FRE_Command_Salan }
				country_event = { id = FRE_Command.5 days = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				country_event = { id = FRE_Command.1 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Endings / dissolution
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

test_fre_win_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = {
			set_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 1500 }
			set_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 100 }
			indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_lose_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = {
			set_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 100 }
			set_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 1500 }
			indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_winddown_now = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_country_flag = FRE_Winddown_Started
			country_event = { id = FRE_Winddown.1 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

test_fre_dissolve_vie = {
	fre_dissolve_cefeo_to_vie = yes
}

test_fre_dissolve_vin = {
	fre_dissolve_cefeo_to_vin = yes
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tai highlands / Dien Bien Phu ownership
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The Northwest campaign now takes only Hoang Lien Son (1761). The Dien Bien Phu
# valley (671, province 4529) changes hands only through the VIN map campaign;
# its objective decides whether the rump federation survives. SWF_Indochina_War.14
# remains the fallback for a game where the CEFEO never fortified the valley.

test_tai_status = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI } log = "TAI STATUS: federation still exists" }
	else = { log = "TAI STATUS: federation is gone" }
	if = { limit = { 1761 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son: VIN" }
	else_if = { limit = { 1761 = { is_owned_by = TAI } } log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son: TAI" }
	else = { log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son: other" }
	if = { limit = { 671 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo (Dien Bien Phu): VIN" }
	else_if = { limit = { 671 = { is_owned_by = TAI } } log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo (Dien Bien Phu): TAI" }
	else = { log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo (Dien Bien Phu): other" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Dien_Bien_Phu_Established } } log = "  camp established: yes" }
	else = { log = "  camp established: no" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed } log = "  GONO garrison: deployed" }
	else = { log = "  GONO garrison: none" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Is_FRE } log = "  GONO owner: FRE" }
	else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed } log = "  GONO owner: TAI" }
	log = "  siege days [?global.VIN_dbp_siege_days|0], works level [?global.VIN_dbp_fort_level|0]"
}

test_vin_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VIN } }
		log = "VIN STATUS: no Viet Minh"
	}
	else = {
		VIN = {
			log = "VIN STATUS: political power [?political_power|0], campaign supply [?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
			if = { limit = { has_idea = extensive_conscription } log = "  conscription: extensive" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_idea = limited_conscription } log = "  conscription: limited (rebalance did not apply)" }
			else = { log = "  conscription: other" }
			if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Resistance_War_Economy } log = "  war economy spirit: on" }
			else = { log = "  war economy spirit: OFF" }
			if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Indochina_AI_Support } log = "  standing AI layer: on" }
			else = { log = "  standing AI layer: off" }
			if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support } log = "  campaign AI layer: on" }
			else = { log = "  campaign AI layer: off" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_CMAG_Arrived } log = "  CMAG: arrived" }
			else = { log = "  CMAG: not yet" }
			log = "  divisions [?num_divisions|0]"
		}
	}
}

test_vin_fence = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VIN } }
		log = "VIN FENCE: no Viet Minh"
	}
	else = {
		VIN = {
			log = "VIN ROUTES: northern campaign connections are unrestricted in map/adjacencies.csv"
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = INDOCHINESE_WAR_DIEN_BIEN_UNLOCKED } log = "  Dien Bien campaign permission for the retained Laos-Vinh crossings: on" }
			else = { log = "  Dien Bien campaign permission for the retained Laos-Vinh crossings: off" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = LAOS_BORDER_UNLOCKED } log = "  Laotian frontier OPEN" }
			else = { log = "  Laotian frontier shut" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active } log = "  valley campaign: in the field" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Dien_Bien } log = "  valley campaign: fought and resolved" }
			else = { log = "  valley campaign: not yet run" }
			if = { limit = { controls_province = 4529 } log = "  4529 Dien Bien Phu: VIN holds it" }
			else = { log = "  4529 Dien Bien Phu: not ours" }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { 671 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo owner: VIN" }
	else_if = { limit = { 671 = { is_owned_by = TAI } } log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo owner: TAI" }
	else = { log = "  671 Tay Bac Bo owner: other" }
	if = { limit = { 1761 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son owner: VIN" }
	else_if = { limit = { 1761 = { is_owned_by = TAI } } log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son owner: TAI" }
	else = { log = "  1761 Hoang Lien Son owner: other" }
}

# Run the scripted siege forward without waiting out the clock. Sets the day
# count one short of each threshold and ticks, so the works come down and, on an
# AI Hanoi, the camp is overrun on the next tick.
#   e test_dbp_siege_advance     jump to day 55 and tick
#   e test_dbp_siege_tick        run one day of the siege now
test_dbp_siege_advance = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 55 }
			vin_dbp_siege_tick = yes
		}
	}
}

test_dbp_siege_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = { vin_dbp_siege_tick = yes }
	}
}

# Expect afterwards: VIN owns 1761, TAI still alive owning 671 and re-puppeted.
test_vin_tay_bac_win = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active
			country_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.5 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# The camp-falls consequence on its own, without playing the whole siege.
test_dbp_tai_collapse = {
	indochina_tai_federation_collapses = yes
	test_tai_status = yes
}

# The no-Castor fallback. Clears the camp flag first so the dispatcher does not
# immediately stand down, and hands VIN the highlands so it can reach the offer.
test_vin_dbp_fallback = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { clr_country_flag = FRE_Dien_Bien_Phu_Established } }
	fre_dbp_disband_gono = yes	# the one-shot garrison guard is global; a re-run would otherwise spawn nothing
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Active
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Tay_Bac
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Hoa_Binh
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Dien_Bien
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Thap_Van_Dai
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Cao_Bac
			if = { limit = { NOT = { owns_state = 1761 } } transfer_state = 1761 }
			country_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.14 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Fill the war chest so every commitment tier is affordable.
test_fre_credits_grant = {

		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 500 }
			fre_change_war_credits = yes
		}
	}
}

# Open the commitment prompt for an operation without taking the focus.
# Defaults to Brochet; edit fre_op_id for the others (2-7). Castor has its own
# exact-province lifecycle and test effects below.
test_fre_operation_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_op_id = 4 }
			fre_operation_begin = yes
		}
	}
}

# Force the watchdog to fire as if the 90-day timer had expired.
test_fre_operation_resolve = {
	fre_operation_resolve = yes
}

# Force the deterministic threshold, skipping the press-or-withdraw prompt.
test_fre_operation_threshold = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_operation_threshold_resolve = yes }
	}
}

# Clear all operation state without firing an outcome.
test_fre_operation_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			fre_operation_finish = yes
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Lorraine_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Hirondelle_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Mouette_Activated
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Brochet_Activated
		}
	}
}

test_fre_operation_status = {
	log = "[FRE OPS] current=[?FRE.FRE_op_current|0] tier=[?FRE.FRE_op_commitment_tier|0] atk=[?FRE.FRE_op_attacker_state|0] def=[?FRE.FRE_op_defender_state|0] credits=[?FRE.FRE_War_Credits|0]"
}

# Start Castor at full commitment. The camp must already be under
# French-aligned control, matching the production focus gate.
test_fre_castor_start_full = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 500 }
			fre_change_war_credits = yes
			add_manpower = 10000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane_equipment amount = 20 producer = FRA }
			set_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Launched
			if = {
				limit = { fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes }
				fre_castor_begin_full_drop = yes
			}
			else = { log = "[CASTOR] BLOCKED - French-aligned forces do not control the camp province" }
		}
	}
}

# Advance one daily check. Set FRE_Castor_Hold_Days to 13 first to exercise
# the objective-complete edge while French-aligned forces control the camp.
test_fre_castor_advance = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_castor_daily_tick = yes }
	}
}

test_fre_castor_day_14 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			FRE = {
				has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Airhead_Active
				fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		FRE = {
			set_variable = { FRE_Castor_Hold_Days = 13 }
			set_variable = { FRE_Castor_Days_Elapsed = 13 }
			fre_castor_daily_tick = yes
		}
	}
}

test_fre_castor_fail = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_castor_resolve_failure = yes }
	}
}

test_fre_castor_supersede = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_castor_resolve_superseded = yes }
	}
}

test_fre_castor_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			log = "[CASTOR] hold=[?FRE_Castor_Hold_Days|0]/[?FRE_Castor_Hold_Required|0] elapsed=[?FRE_Castor_Days_Elapsed|0] credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]"
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Airhead_Active } log = "[CASTOR] Airhead establishment active" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Success } log = "[CASTOR] Result: airhead established" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure } log = "[CASTOR] Result: establishment failed" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Superseded } log = "[CASTOR] Result: theatre-superseded" }
			set_temp_variable = { fre_castor_test_results = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Success } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_castor_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_castor_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_castor_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fre_castor_test_results > 1 } } log = "[CASTOR] FAIL - more than one permanent result" }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Airhead_Active
					NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Castor_Activated }
				}
				log = "[CASTOR] FAIL - active airhead has no active mission flag"
			}
		}
	}
}

test_fre_castor_reset = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			fre_castor_force_superseded_cleanup = yes
			fre_dbp_disband_gono = yes
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Success
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Superseded
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure_Full
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure_Limited
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Unfunded
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Landing_Ground_Lost
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Success
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Failure
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Castor_Completed
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Castor_Failed
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Launched
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Cooldown_Castor
			clr_country_flag = FRE_Dien_Bien_Phu_Established
		}
	}
}

test_fre_pollux_prepare = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 500 }
			fre_change_war_credits = yes
			add_manpower = 10000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane_equipment amount = 20 producer = FRA }
			set_country_flag = FRE_Dien_Bien_Phu_Established
			set_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Launched
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed }
					fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes
				}
				fre_dbp_fortify_camp = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

test_fre_pollux_start_historical = {

			country_exists = FRE

				fre_pollux_live_window_trigger = yes
				fre_pollux_french_aligned_controls_lai_chau_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		FRE = { fre_pollux_begin_historical = yes }
	}
	else = { log = "[POLLUX] BLOCKED - camp or Lai Chau is not under French-aligned control" }
}

test_fre_pollux_day_7 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			FRE = {
				has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Escort  fre_pollux_corridor_open_trigger = yes }
					AND = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Airlift  fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		FRE = {
			set_variable = { FRE_Pollux_Hold_Days = 6 }
			set_variable = { FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed = 6 }
			fre_pollux_daily_tick = yes
		}
	}
	else = { log = "[POLLUX] BLOCKED - selected evacuation objective is not controlled" }
}

		limit = { country_exists = FRE }

	}
}

test_fre_pollux_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			log = "[POLLUX] hold=[?FRE_Pollux_Hold_Days|0]/7 elapsed=[?FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed|0]/21 credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]"
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active } log = "[POLLUX] Evacuation active" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Historical } log = "[POLLUX] Historical split evacuation" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Escort } log = "[POLLUX] Route Pavie escort" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Airlift } log = "[POLLUX] Expanded airlift" }
			if = { limit = { fre_pollux_french_aligned_controls_lai_chau_trigger = yes } log = "[POLLUX] Lai Chau controlled" }
			if = { limit = { fre_pollux_french_aligned_controls_route_pavie_trigger = yes } log = "[POLLUX] Route Pavie approach controlled" }
			if = { limit = { fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes } log = "[POLLUX] Dien Bien Phu camp controlled" }
			set_temp_variable = { fre_pollux_test_results = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Preserved } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_pollux_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_pollux_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_pollux_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fre_pollux_test_results > 1 } } log = "[POLLUX] FAIL - more than one permanent result" }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active
					NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Pollux_Activated }
				}
				log = "[POLLUX] FAIL - active evacuation has no mission flag"
			}
		}
	}
}

test_fre_atlante_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			log = "[ATLANTE] hold=[?FRE_Atlante_Hold_Days|0]/14 elapsed=[?FRE_Atlante_Days_Elapsed|0]/90 credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0] dbp_siege=[?global.VIN_dbp_siege_days|0]"
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active } log = "[ATLANTE] Offensive active" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Full } log = "[ATLANTE] Full CEFEO commitment" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Vietnamese } log = "[ATLANTE] Vietnamese-led commitment" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Strategy_Northern_Priority } log = "[ATLANTE] Northern priority selected" }
			if = { limit = { fre_atlante_french_aligned_controls_north_objective_trigger = yes } log = "[ATLANTE] Northern Interzone V objective controlled" }
			if = { limit = { fre_atlante_french_aligned_controls_south_objective_trigger = yes } log = "[ATLANTE] Southern Interzone V objective controlled" }
			if = { limit = { fre_atlante_vietnamese_objective_trigger = yes } log = "[ATLANTE] Vietnamese coastal objective controlled" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Northern_Reserve_Available } log = "[ATLANTE] Northern reserve available" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Northern_Reserve_Committed } log = "[ATLANTE] Northern reserve committed at Dien Bien Phu" }
			set_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Central_Success } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Vietnamese_Foothold } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Stalled } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Northern_Priority } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { fre_atlante_test_results > 1 } } log = "[ATLANTE] FAIL - more than one permanent result" }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active
					NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Atlante_Activated }
				}
				log = "[ATLANTE] FAIL - active offensive has no mission flag"
			}
		}
	}
}

test_fre_lorraine_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			log = "[LORRAINE] credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0] hold_days=[?FRE_Lorraine_Objective_Hold_Days|0]"
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Active } log = "[LORRAINE] Response active" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Deep } log = "[LORRAINE] Deep-thrust posture" }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Objective_Achieved } log = "[LORRAINE] Rear-depot objective achieved" }
			set_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Success } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Limited } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Failure } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Withheld } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Resolved
					NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Active }
					NOT = { check_variable = { fre_lorraine_test_results = 1 } }
				}
				log = "[LORRAINE] FAIL - resolved response does not have exactly one result flag"
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

test_fre_limited_operation_status = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			log = "[FRE LIMITED] active=[?FRE_op_current|0] day=[?FRE_op_days_elapsed|0] hold=[?FRE_op_objective_hold_days|0] outcome=[?FRE_op_last_outcome|0]"
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active
					country_exists = VIN
					VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending }
				}
				log = "[FRE LIMITED] FAIL - VIN campaign armistice retry overlaps the live operation"
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active }
					OR = { has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Light has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Standard has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Maximum }
				}
				log = "[FRE LIMITED] FAIL - commitment idea survived cleanup"
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active FRE_no_operation_running_trigger = yes }
				log = "[FRE LIMITED] FAIL - shared operation gate does not see the live operation"
			}
		}
	}
}

		limit = { country_exists = FRE }

			add_ideas = FRE_Limited_Operation_Standard
			set_temp_variable = { fre_op_outcome = 5 }
			fre_operation_resolve_outcome = yes
			test_fre_limited_operation_status = yes
		}
	}
}

test_geneva_reset = {
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded
	clr_global_flag = geneva_unified_settlement
	clr_global_flag = geneva_interim_government_period
	clr_global_flag = geneva_election_resolved
	clr_global_flag = Geneva_Conference_Preparations
	clr_global_flag = Geneva_Conference_Negotiations_Complete
	clr_global_flag = Geneva_Conference
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conf_attendance_autogranted	# otherwise a re-convened conference silently pays no delegation payload
	clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit_Source = 0 }
	clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Panic
	clr_global_flag = geneva_usa_panic_no_ballot
	clr_global_flag = geneva_election_campaign_open
	clr_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
	set_variable = { global.geneva_round = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_tick_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_outcome = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_wrecker = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_partition_line = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_elections_term = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_withdrawal_term = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_alignment_term = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_laos_cambodia_term = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_minorities_term = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_final_comm_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_final_anticomm_score = 0 }
	clear_array = global.geneva_delegations
	clear_array = global.geneva_clause_current
	clear_array = global.geneva_push
	clear_array = global.geneva_severity
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_country
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_clause
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_option
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_amount
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_days
	clear_array = global.geneva_neg_clause_count
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				tag = FRA
				tag = VIE
				tag = VIN
				tag = USA
				tag = SOV
				tag = PRC
				tag = ENG
				tag = LOS
				tag = CAM
				tag = LAO
				tag = PQC
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = geneva_walked_out
		clr_country_flag = geneva_core_delegation
		set_variable = { View_Geneva_Conference = 0 }
		set_variable = { geneva_grievance = 0 }
		set_variable = { geneva_neg_display_clause = 0 }
		set_variable = { geneva_neg_display_days = 0 }
		clear_array = geneva_neg_mine
	}
	every_country = {	# reseating re-adds it; a mid-session reset must not leave the button behind
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = country_politics_list value = 29 } }
		remove_from_array = { array = country_politics_list value = 29 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = {
			clr_country_flag = FRA_Accepted_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = FRA_Rejected_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = FRA_Accepted_Reunification
			clr_country_flag = FRA_Rejected_Reunification
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		VIE = {
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Accepted_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Rejected_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Accepted_Reunification
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Rejected_Reunification
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Rejected_Reunification_USA_Intervention
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Accepted_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Rejected_Ceasefire
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Accepted_Reunification
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Rejected_Reunification
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = USA }
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = USA_Tampered_With_VIE
			clr_country_flag = USA_geneva_leaned_on_saigon
		}
	}
}

test_geneva_debug = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_unified_settlement }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_interim_government_period }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_election_resolved }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference_Negotiations_Complete }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.geneva_round > 0 } }
	}
	else = {
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { global.geneva_delegations = THIS.id } }
	}
	else = {
	}
}

test_geneva_neg_debug = {
	log = "GENEVA NEG: entries=[?global.geneva_neg_country^num]"
	set_temp_variable = { g_dbg_i = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { g_dbg_sz = global.geneva_neg_country^num }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { g_dbg_i < g_dbg_sz } }
		log = "GENEVA NEG [?g_dbg_i|0]: country=[?global.geneva_neg_country^g_dbg_i|0] clause=[?global.geneva_neg_clause^g_dbg_i|0] option=[?global.geneva_neg_option^g_dbg_i|0] amount=[?global.geneva_neg_amount^g_dbg_i|0] days=[?global.geneva_neg_days^g_dbg_i|0]"
		add_to_temp_variable = { g_dbg_i = 1 }
	}
}

test_geneva_runoff = {
	test_geneva_force_session = yes
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { global.geneva_delegations = THIS.id } }
		set_variable = { geneva_prio^1 = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { global.geneva_round = 0 }	# keep the tick from tripping the round-10 compile
	set_variable = { global.geneva_clause_current^1 = 2 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^5 = 1000 }	# clause 1, option 1 (13th parallel): the plurality
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^6 = 600 }	# clause 1, option 2 (17th parallel)
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^7 = 500 }	# clause 1, option 3 (20th parallel)
	geneva_conf_round_tick = yes
	log = "GENEVA RUNOFF: 13th=1000 vs 17th+20th=1100 -> line=[?global.geneva_clause_current^1|0] (expect 2)"
}

test_geneva_runoff_20th = {
	test_geneva_force_session = yes
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { global.geneva_delegations = THIS.id } }
		set_variable = { geneva_prio^1 = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { global.geneva_round = 0 }	# keep the tick from tripping the round-10 compile
	set_variable = { global.geneva_clause_current^1 = 2 }
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^5 = 500 }	# clause 1, option 1 (13th parallel)
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^6 = 600 }	# clause 1, option 2 (17th parallel)
	set_variable = { global.geneva_push^7 = 1000 }	# clause 1, option 3 (20th parallel): the plurality
	geneva_conf_round_tick = yes
	log = "GENEVA RUNOFF 20TH: 20th=1000 vs 13th+17th=1100 -> line=[?global.geneva_clause_current^1|0] (expect 2)"
}

test_geneva_fre_pursuit = {
	test_geneva_reset = yes
	set_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 5 }
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 0 }
	indochina_geneva_pursuit_tick = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced }
		log = "GENEVA PURSUIT: FAIL - convened at phase 5"
	}
	else = {
		log = "GENEVA PURSUIT: deferred at phase 5, B=[?global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points|0] (expect 200)"
	}
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 8 }
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 600 }
	indochina_geneva_pursuit_tick = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced }
		log = "GENEVA PURSUIT: convened at phase 8 as expected, pursuit flag cleared"
	}
	else = {
		log = "GENEVA PURSUIT: FAIL - still deferred at phase 8 with B=600"
	}
}

		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded }

	}
}

		limit = { geneva_panic_collapse_trigger = yes }
		indochina_geneva_panic_force_conference = yes

	}
	else = {
		log = "GENEVA PANIC: FAIL - trigger did not fire"
	}
}

test_geneva_campaign_window = {
	log = "GENEVA CAMPAIGN WINDOW: term=[?global.geneva_elections_term|0] interim=[?global.geneva_tick_days|0]"
	if = {
		limit = { geneva_election_campaign_open_trigger = yes }
		log = "GENEVA CAMPAIGN WINDOW: already open"
	}
	else = {
		log = "GENEVA CAMPAIGN WINDOW: shut (expected until the run-up) - opening it now"
	}
	set_global_flag = geneva_election_campaign_open
	if = {
		limit = { geneva_election_campaign_open_trigger = yes }
		log = "GENEVA CAMPAIGN WINDOW: open - the ballot focuses should now be selectable"
	}
	else = {
		log = "GENEVA CAMPAIGN WINDOW: FAIL - still shut with the flag set, so the pending half is false"
	}
}

test_geneva_unified_liquidation = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: FAIL - FRE still exists"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NUN }
		log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: FAIL - NUN still exists"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI }
		log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: FAIL - TAI still exists"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAM }
		log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: FAIL - TAM still exists"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = MEO
			NOT = { check_variable = { global.geneva_minorities_term = 1 } }
		}
		log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: FAIL - MEO survived without a minorities clause"
	}
	log = "GENEVA UNIFIED: liquidation check complete"
}

test_vie_daiviet_plot = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		VIE = {
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = VIE_DaiViet_Plot_Running
				days = 120
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { VIE_DaiViet_Threat > 99 } }
				country_event = DaiViet.3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { VIE_DaiViet_Threat > 74 } }
				country_event = DaiViet.2
			}
			else = {
				country_event = DaiViet.1
			}
			log = "VIE DAIVIET: plot fired at threat=[?VIE_DaiViet_Threat|0]"
		}
	}
}

			if = {

				limit = { has_country_flag = VIE_Hinh_Coup }
				country_event = Diem.62
			}
			else = {
				country_event = Diem.61
			}
			log = "VIE END: end of content event fired"
		}
	}
}
