ic_afk_validation_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Validation_Initialized } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Validation_Initialized
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_heartbeat_days = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_days = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_signature = -1 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_pollux_stage_last = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_atlante_stage_last = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_garrison_check_wait = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_communist = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_france = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_independence = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_ethnic = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore }
		log = "IC_AFK|START|date=[GetDateText]|mode=passive_production_observer|game_log_filter=IC_AFK"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_score_delta_tick = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_communist = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_communist = global.ic_afk_prev_communist }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_france = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_france = global.ic_afk_prev_pro_france }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_independence = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_independence = global.ic_afk_prev_pro_independence }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic = global.ic_afk_prev_pro_ethnic }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_communist = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_france = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_independence = 0 } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic = 0 } }
			}
		}
		log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_DELTA|date=[GetDateText]|source=daily_ledger_observer|communist=[?ic_afk_delta_communist|0]|pro_france=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_france|0]|pro_independence=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_independence|0]|pro_ethnic=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic|0]|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]"
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_communist = global.ic_afk_traced_communist }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_france = global.ic_afk_traced_pro_france }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_independence = global.ic_afk_traced_pro_independence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic = global.ic_afk_traced_pro_ethnic }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_communist = 0 } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_france = 0 } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_independence = 0 } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic = 0 } }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_UNTRACED|date=[GetDateText]|communist=[?ic_afk_delta_communist|0]|pro_france=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_france|0]|pro_independence=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_independence|0]|pro_ethnic=[?ic_afk_delta_pro_ethnic|0]"
		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_communist = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_france = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_independence = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_prev_pro_ethnic = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_communist = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_france = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_independence = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_ethnic = 0 }
}

ic_afk_trace_score_communist = {
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_communist = ic_trace_amount }
	log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_WRITE|date=[GetDateText]|track=communist|amount=[?ic_trace_amount|0]|writer=[?ic_trace_writer|0]|root=[ROOT.GetTag]"
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 0 }
}

ic_afk_trace_score_pro_france = {
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_france = ic_trace_amount }
	log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_WRITE|date=[GetDateText]|track=pro_france|amount=[?ic_trace_amount|0]|writer=[?ic_trace_writer|0]|root=[ROOT.GetTag]"
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 0 }
}

ic_afk_trace_score_pro_independence = {
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_independence = ic_trace_amount }
	log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_WRITE|date=[GetDateText]|track=pro_independence|amount=[?ic_trace_amount|0]|writer=[?ic_trace_writer|0]|root=[ROOT.GetTag]"
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 0 }
}

ic_afk_trace_score_pro_ethnic = {
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_traced_pro_ethnic = ic_trace_amount }
	log = "IC_AFK|SCORE_WRITE|date=[GetDateText]|track=pro_ethnic|amount=[?ic_trace_amount|0]|writer=[?ic_trace_writer|0]|root=[ROOT.GetTag]"
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 0 }
}

ic_afk_trace_score_pair = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pair_writer = ic_trace_writer }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 0 } } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_amount = ic_trace_communist }
		ic_afk_trace_score_communist = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = 0 } } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = ic_trace_pair_writer }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_amount = ic_trace_pro_france }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pro_france = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = 0 }
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_idea = VIN_Indochina_AI_Support has_idea = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support } }
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_hidden_AI_buffs_removed|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
	}
	else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_hidden_AI_buffs_removed|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]" }
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			has_war_with = FRE
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } country_exists = TAM has_war_with = TAM }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } country_exists = THO has_war_with = THO }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI }
			}
			OR = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } NOT = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } NOT = { country_exists = THO } THO = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } NOT = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		}
		log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_campaign_participant_envelope|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
	}
	else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_campaign_participant_envelope|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]" }
	clr_global_flag = IC_AFK_VIN_Overextension_Was_Active
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_post_dbp_strategy_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Strategy_Briefed NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Strategy_Briefing } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Strategy_Briefing
		log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=briefed|dbp=[?global.geneva_outcome_dbp|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Negotiations_Chosen NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Negotiations } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Negotiations
		log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|strategy=negotiations|queue_owner=VIN_focus|peace_gate_pending=yes"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Escalation } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Escalation
		log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|strategy=escalation|panic_suppressed=yes|nlf_annexed=no"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Launched NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva
		log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|strategy=negotiations|focus_queue=consumed|peace_gate=passed"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Invasion_Declared NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Invasion } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Post_DBP_Invasion
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE NOT = { has_global_flag = Vietnam_War } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=post_DBP_direct_VIN_VIE_invasion_without_1960s_system" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=post_DBP_direct_VIN_VIE_invasion_without_1960s_system" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_launch_tick = {
	ic_afk_validation_initialize = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_4_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_4_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=4|name=Cao_Bac|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = THO }
			THO = {
				if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1280 size > 0 } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=THO_Cao_Bang_garrison_present" }
				else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=THO_Cao_Bang_garrison_present" }
				if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1768 size > 0 } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=THO_Lang_Son_garrison_present" }
				else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=THO_Lang_Son_garrison_present" }
			}
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=THO_exists_at_Cao_Bac_launch" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_1_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_1_Launch
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nghia_lo_bonus = 0 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Nghia_Lo_Result_Clean has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Nghia_Lo_Result_Costly } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nghia_lo_bonus = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=1|name=Northwest|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]|nghia_lo_bonus=[?ic_afk_nghia_lo_bonus|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_2_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_2_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=2|name=Hoa_Binh|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_3_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_3_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=3|name=Dien_Bien_Phu|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Atlante_remains_active_at_DBP_launch" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Vinh_Yen_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_5_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_5_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=5|name=Vinh_Yen|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Vinh_Yen } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Vinh_Yen_objective_marker_active|province=12075" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Vinh_Yen_objective_marker_active|province=12075" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Mao_Khe_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_6_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_6_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=6|name=Mao_Khe|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Mao_Khe } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mao_Khe_objective_marker_active|province=13772" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mao_Khe_objective_marker_active|province=13772" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Day_River_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_7_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_7_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=7|name=Day_River|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Day_River } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Day_River_objective_markers_active|provinces=1185,13753,13755" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Day_River_objective_markers_active|provinces=1185,13753,13755" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Na_San_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_8_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_8_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=8|name=Na_San|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Na_San } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Na_San_objective_marker_active|province=13757" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Na_San_objective_marker_active|province=13757" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Nghia_Lo_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_10_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_10_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=10|name=Nghia_Lo|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_clean=[?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0]|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_balance_contract = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Nghia_Lo } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Nghia_Lo_objective_marker_active|province=13773" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Nghia_Lo_objective_marker_active|province=13773" }
	}
	ic_afk_validation_vin_lai_chau_launch = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_9_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_VIN_9_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=9|name=Northwest_Recovery|hold=[?VIN_camp_objective_hold_days|0]/5|deadline_final=[?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]|pp=[?political_power|0]"
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Attempted } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Northwest_recovery_attempt_locked_at_launch" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Northwest_recovery_attempt_locked_at_launch" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_VIN_Overextension_Was_Active } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_VIN_Overextension_Was_Active
		log = "IC_AFK|ENVELOPE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|status=severe_penalty_applied|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_envelope_cause = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } has_global_flag = IC_AFK_VIN_Overextension_Was_Active }
		clr_global_flag = IC_AFK_VIN_Overextension_Was_Active
		log = "IC_AFK|ENVELOPE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|status=penalty_cleared|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
	}
}

# Which foreign ground tripped the ordinary VIN overextension tier. Bits:
# 1 Cao-Bac, 2 Nung, 4 Ha Giang, 8 Northwest corridor, 16 Northwest outlier,
# 32 Hoa Binh, 64 Dien Bien, 128 Delta, 256 a CEFEO operation owns the penalty.
# The generic ground triggers are logged, so a bit can also be set for ground
# the running campaign excepts; read it against the campaign number.
ic_afk_validation_vin_envelope_cause = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 0 }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_cao_bac_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_nung_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_ha_giang_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 4 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_northwest_corridor_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 8 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_northwest_outlier_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 16 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_hoa_binh_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 32 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_dien_bien_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 64 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_controls_foreign_delta_ground_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 128 } }
	if = { limit = { vin_fre_operation_overextended_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_env_bits = 256 } }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 0 }
	if = { limit = { 1760 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = VIN } } controls_province = 4075 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 1 } }
	if = {
		limit = { 786 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = VIN } } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 1185 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 4119 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 13753 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 8 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 13755 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 16 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 13756 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 32 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 13770 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 64 } }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 13772 } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_delta_prov = 128 } }
	}
	log = "IC_AFK|ENVELOPE_CAUSE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|bits=[?ic_afk_env_bits|0]|delta_provinces=[?ic_afk_delta_prov|0]|streak_days=[?VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days|0]|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]"
}

# Ground held when the severe tier applied. Bits: 1 Luang Prabang state (1198;
# informational only, it no longer causes the severe tier), 2 Vientiane state
# (670), 4 delta core (4075/4119) outside campaigns 5-7, 8 the all-in corridor.
# raid=1 while the Pathet Lao raid is live. Bit 1 alone means an unknown cause.
ic_afk_validation_vin_severe_cause = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_bits = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active }
			1198 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = VIN } }
			OR = {
				controls_province = 4613
				controls_province = 7075
				controls_province = 7103
				controls_province = 10496
				controls_province = 13733
				controls_province = 13738
				controls_province = 13740
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_bits = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			670 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = VIN } }
			OR = {
				controls_province = 1464
				controls_province = 1507
				controls_province = 13342
				controls_province = 13751
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_bits = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } }
			vin_controls_foreign_delta_core_trigger = yes
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_bits = 4 }
	}
	if = { limit = { vin_all_in_corridor_overextended_trigger = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_bits = 8 } }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_raid = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_sev_raid = 1 } }
	log = "IC_AFK|SEVERE_CAUSE|date=[GetDateText]|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|bits=[?ic_afk_sev_bits|0]|raid=[?ic_afk_sev_raid|0]"
}

# A theatre country capitulated. ROOT is the capitulated country, FROM the
# winner. Not gated on Indochina_War_Over: same-named on_action slots merge
# across files in no guaranteed order, so the failsafe may already have ended
# the war in this frame.
ic_afk_validation_capitulation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = indochina_struggle_initialized
			ROOT = { ic_failsafe_theatre_tag_trigger = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_cap_overext = 0 }
		if = { limit = { ROOT = { OR = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe } } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_cap_overext = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_cap_capital_held = 1 }
		if = { limit = { ROOT = { capital_scope = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT } } } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_cap_capital_held = 0 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|CAPITULATION|date=[GetDateText]|tag=[ROOT.GetTag]|winner=[FROM.GetTag]|divisions=[?ROOT.num_divisions|0]|owned_controlled_states=[?ROOT.num_owned_controlled_states|0]|capital_held=[?ic_afk_cap_capital_held|0]|overextended=[?ic_afk_cap_overext|0]|vin_campaign=[?ROOT.VIN_camp_current|0]"
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_reason = 3 }
			ROOT = { ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_log = yes }
		}
	}
}

# Viet Minh surrender progress and victory-point ground while at war with
# CEFEO. VIN scope. Logs on the first war day, whenever the bucket or the lost
# ground changes, and weekly. reason: 1 change, 2 weekly, 3 capitulation.
ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = FRE }
		ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_measure = yes
		add_to_variable = { ic_afk_surr_days = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_reason = 0 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_surr_days > 6 } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_reason = 2 } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = ic_afk_surr_last_pct }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_surr_last_pct = ic_afk_surr_pct } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ic_afk_surr_last_lost = ic_afk_vp_lost } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_reason = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_surr_reason > 0 } }
			set_variable = { ic_afk_surr_days = 0 }
			set_variable = { ic_afk_surr_last_pct = ic_afk_surr_pct }
			set_variable = { ic_afk_surr_last_lost = ic_afk_vp_lost }
			ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_write = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = ic_afk_surr_last_pct }
		clear_variable = ic_afk_surr_last_pct
		clear_variable = ic_afk_surr_last_lost
		set_variable = { ic_afk_surr_days = 0 }
	}
}

# Caller sets ic_afk_surr_reason.
ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_log = {
	ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_measure = yes
	ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_write = yes
}

# surrender_pct is the engine surrender progress floored to 5%.
# VP bits (province, state, value): 1 7015 881 5, 2 11936 1762 3,
# 4 4397 1763 5, 8 12297 838 1, 16 17194 1875 1, 32 17193 1875 1,
# 64 12065 1280 10, 128 13767 1280 1, 256 13765 671 6, 512 4529 671 5,
# 1024 10129 1766 5, 2048 7093 1281 5, 4096 4075 1760 20, 8192 4119 786 10.
# vp_owned: VIN owns the state. vp_lost: VIN owns the state, not the province.
ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_measure = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 0 }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.05 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 5 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.10 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.15 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 15 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.20 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 20 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.25 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 25 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.30 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 30 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.35 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 35 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.40 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 40 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.45 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 45 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.50 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 50 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.55 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 55 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.60 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 60 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.65 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 65 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.70 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 70 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.75 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 75 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.80 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 80 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.85 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 85 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.90 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 90 } }
	if = { limit = { surrender_progress > 0.95 } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_pct = 95 } }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 0 }
	if = { limit = { 881 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 1 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 7015 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 1 } } }
	if = { limit = { 1762 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 2 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 11936 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 2 } } }
	if = { limit = { 1763 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 4 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 4397 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 4 } } }
	if = { limit = { 838 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 8 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 12297 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 8 } } }
	if = {
		limit = { 1875 = { is_owned_by = VIN } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 48 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 17194 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 16 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 17193 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 32 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 1280 = { is_owned_by = VIN } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 192 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 12065 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 64 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 13767 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 128 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 671 = { is_owned_by = VIN } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 768 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 13765 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 256 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 4529 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 512 } }
	}
	if = { limit = { 1766 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 1024 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 10129 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 1024 } } }
	if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 2048 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 7093 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 2048 } } }
	if = { limit = { 1760 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 4096 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 4075 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 4096 } } }
	if = { limit = { 786 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_owned = 8192 } if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_province = 4119 } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_vp_lost = 8192 } } }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_capital = 1 }
	if = { limit = { capital_scope = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = VIN } } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_surr_capital = 0 } }
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_write = {
	log = "IC_AFK|SURRENDER|date=[GetDateText]|reason=[?ic_afk_surr_reason|0]|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|surrender_pct=[?ic_afk_surr_pct|0]|vp_owned=[?ic_afk_vp_owned|0]|vp_lost=[?ic_afk_vp_lost|0]|capital_held=[?ic_afk_surr_capital|0]|states=[?num_owned_controlled_states|0]|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]"
}

# Why the monthly northern resupply for the southern Viet Minh did not run.
# VIN scope. Gate bits: 1 Geneva concluded, 2 post-DBP stand-down ordered,
# 4 NLF missing, 8 NLF not at war, 16 NLF has the post-DBP supply line,
# 32 VIN Campaign Supply below 50. No line at all means the monthly accrual
# never called the resupply.
ic_afk_validation_nlf_resupply_skip = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Ordered } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = NLF } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 4 } }
	else = {
		if = { limit = { NLF = { has_war = no } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 8 } }
		if = { limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 16 } }
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_Campaign_Supply > 49 } } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_nlf_gates = 32 } }
	log = "IC_AFK|NLF_RESUPPLY_SKIP|date=[GetDateText]|gates=[?ic_afk_nlf_gates|0]|vin_supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_result_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Lai_Chau } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lai_Chau_objective_marker_active|province=13765" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lai_Chau_objective_marker_active|province=13765" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_vin_cleanup_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending } }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active } }
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_campaign_cleanup_handoff_to_FRE_operation|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign|0]"
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 0 }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE has_war_with = FRE } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA has_war_with = FRA } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM has_war_with = TAM } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = THO has_war_with = THO } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN has_war_with = NUN } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_remaining_wars = 0 } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_campaign_full_armistice|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign|0]|remaining_french_aligned_wars=0" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=VIN_campaign_full_armistice|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign|0]|remaining_french_aligned_wars=[?ic_afk_remaining_wars|0]" }
		}
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_vin_cleanup_campaign = 0 }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_fre_balance_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VIN
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { NOT = { has_war_with = VIN } } }
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { NOT = { has_war_with = VIN } } }
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { NOT = { has_war_with = VIN } } }
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = THO } THO = { NOT = { has_war_with = VIN } } }
		}
		log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_participant_envelope|operation=[?FRE_op_current|0]"
	}
	else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_participant_envelope|operation=[?FRE_op_current|0]" }
	clr_global_flag = IC_AFK_FRE_Overextension_Was_Active
}

ic_afk_validation_fre_limited_tick = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_fre_result_new = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 2 } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_2_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_2_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|tier=[?FRE_op_commitment_tier|0]|deadline=[?FRE_op_deadline_y|0]"
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_blocked_during_Laos_raid|operation=2" }
		ic_afk_validation_fre_balance_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 3 } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_3_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_3_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|tier=[?FRE_op_commitment_tier|0]|deadline=[?FRE_op_deadline_y|0]"
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_blocked_during_Laos_raid|operation=3" }
		ic_afk_validation_fre_balance_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 4 } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_4_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_4_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|tier=[?FRE_op_commitment_tier|0]|deadline=[?FRE_op_deadline_y|0]"
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_blocked_during_Laos_raid|operation=4" }
		ic_afk_validation_fre_balance_contract = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = FRE_Operation_Overextension NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_FRE_Overextension_Was_Active } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_FRE_Overextension_Was_Active
		log = "IC_AFK|ENVELOPE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|status=severe_penalty_applied|operation=[?FRE_op_current|0]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Operation_Overextension } has_global_flag = IC_AFK_FRE_Overextension_Was_Active }
		clr_global_flag = IC_AFK_FRE_Overextension_Was_Active
		log = "IC_AFK|ENVELOPE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|status=penalty_cleared|operation=[?FRE_op_current|0]"
	}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Operation_Armistice_Overlap } } set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Operation_Armistice_Overlap log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_not_overlapped_by_VIN_armistice_retry|operation=[?FRE_op_current|0]" }

	if = {
		limit = { FRE_hirondelle_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_2_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_2_Result
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_fre_result_new = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Hirondelle_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|outcome=clean" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Hirondelle_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|outcome=costly" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Hirondelle_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|outcome=aborted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Hirondelle_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Hirondelle_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=2|name=Hirondelle|outcome=superseded" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { FRE_mouette_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_3_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_3_Result
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_fre_result_new = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Mouette_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|outcome=clean" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Mouette_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|outcome=costly" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Mouette_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|outcome=aborted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Mouette_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Mouette_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=3|name=Mouette|outcome=superseded" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { FRE_brochet_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_4_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_4_Result
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_fre_result_new = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|outcome=clean" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|outcome=costly" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|outcome=aborted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=4|name=Brochet|outcome=superseded" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active } OR = { FRE_hirondelle_terminal_trigger = yes FRE_mouette_terminal_trigger = yes FRE_brochet_terminal_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Light has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Standard has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Maximum } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_FRE_Commitment_Cleanup } } set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_FRE_Commitment_Cleanup log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_commitment_cleanup" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_fre_result_new = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 0 }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN has_war_with = VIN } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI TAI = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM TAM = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN NUN = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO THO = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Owns_War } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=0" }
		else_if = {
			# A superseding VIN campaign declares its own war in the same tick; those wars belong to it.
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Owns_War } country_exists = VIN VIN = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } } }
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=0|vin_campaign_wars=[?ic_afk_operation_wars|0]|vin_campaign=[?VIN.VIN_camp_current|0]"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=[?ic_afk_operation_wars|0]" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_laos_raid_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Laos_Raid_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Laos_Raid_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Pathet_Lao_Raid|timer_owner=FRA|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active check_variable = { global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed > 95 } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Laos_Raid_Stuck } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Laos_Raid_Stuck
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pathet_Lao_raid_resolves_on_clock|stage=initial|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Last_Chance check_variable = { global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed > 50 } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Laos_Last_Chance_Stuck } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_Laos_Last_Chance_Stuck
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pathet_Lao_raid_resolves_on_clock|stage=last_chance|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Laos_Raid_Launch has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Resolved NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Laos_Raid_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Laos_Raid_Result
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Total_War } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Pathet_Lao_Raid|outcome=total_war|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Attacker_Won } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Pathet_Lao_Raid|outcome=attacker_victory|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Stalemate } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Pathet_Lao_Raid|outcome=stalemate|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Total_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Pathet_Lao_Raid|outcome=total_failure|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pathet_Lao_raid_has_terminal_outcome|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_luang_prabang_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_LP_All_In_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_LP_All_In_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|timer_owner=FRA|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe
					NOT = { vin_all_in_corridor_overextended_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { vin_campaign_severe_overextension_trigger = yes }
				}
				log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=LP_all_in_severe_tier_has_cause"
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor } }
				log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=LP_all_in_corridor_flag_present"
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe } }
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=overextension_tiers_never_stack"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension_severe } } } }
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=raid_modifier_suspended_under_severe"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_LP_All_In_Launch has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Resolved NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_LP_All_In_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_LP_All_In_Result
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VIN }
			VIN = {
				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Success } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|outcome=success|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
				else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|outcome=failure|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
				else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|outcome=superseded|day=[?global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration|0]" }
				else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=LP_all_in_has_terminal_outcome" }
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor }
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe }
							vin_campaign_severe_overextension_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=LP_all_in_cleanup"
				}
				else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=LP_all_in_cleanup" }
			}
		}
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_fre_late_packages_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Airhead_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Castor_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Castor_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Castor|full_price=[?FRE_Castor_Full_Price|0]|limited_price=[?FRE_Castor_Limited_Price|0]|hold_required=[?FRE_Castor_Hold_Required|0]"
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Brochet|result=clean|discount=15" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Brochet_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Brochet|result=costly|discount=5" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Brochet|result=no_discount" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Na_San_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Na_San|result=clean|discount=15" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Na_San_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Na_San|result=costly|discount=5" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Na_San_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Na_San|result=failure|surcharge=15" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Na_San|result=no_adjustment" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Luang_Prabang_Callback } log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Luang_Prabang|result=applied|discount=15" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Success has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Superseded } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Castor_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Castor_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Success } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Castor|outcome=success" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Castor|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Castor|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Airhead_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Castor_active_flag_cleanup" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Castor_active_flag_cleanup" }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Launch
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Historical } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|posture=historical_split|hold_required=7|deadline=21" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Escort } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|posture=escort|hold_required=7|deadline=21" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Airlift } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|posture=airlift|hold_required=7|deadline=21" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pollux_posture_selected" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Preserved has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Superseded } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Preserved } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|outcome=preserved" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|outcome=column_loss" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Pollux|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pollux_active_flag_cleanup" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Pollux_active_flag_cleanup" }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Launch
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Full } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|posture=full|hold_required=14|deadline=90" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Vietnamese } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|posture=vietnamese_led|hold_required=14|deadline=90" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Atlante_posture_selected" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Central_Success has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Vietnamese_Foothold has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Stalled has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Northern_Priority has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Superseded } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Central_Success } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|outcome=central_success" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Vietnamese_Foothold } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|outcome=vietnamese_foothold" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Stalled } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|outcome=stalled" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Northern_Priority } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|outcome=northern_priority" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Atlante|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Atlante_theatre_cleanup" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_fre_stage_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Evacuation_Active }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap = FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap = global.ic_afk_pollux_stage_last }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap > 6 } }
			set_variable = { global.ic_afk_pollux_stage_last = FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed }
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Escort fre_pollux_corridor_open_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Airlift fre_dbp_french_aligned_controls_camp_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Historical } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|day=[?FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?FRE_Pollux_Hold_Days|0]|posture=historical_split|objective_held=[?ic_afk_stage_objective|0]" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Posture_Escort } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|day=[?FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?FRE_Pollux_Hold_Days|0]|posture=escort|objective_held=[?ic_afk_stage_objective|0]" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|day=[?FRE_Pollux_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?FRE_Pollux_Hold_Days|0]|posture=airlift|objective_held=[?ic_afk_stage_objective|0]" }
		}
	}
	else = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_pollux_stage_last = 0 } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap = FRE_Atlante_Days_Elapsed }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap = global.ic_afk_atlante_stage_last }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_stage_gap > 6 } }
			set_variable = { global.ic_afk_atlante_stage_last = FRE_Atlante_Days_Elapsed }
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 0 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Full fre_atlante_full_objective_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Vietnamese fre_atlante_vietnamese_objective_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_stage_objective = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Posture_Full } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|day=[?FRE_Atlante_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?FRE_Atlante_Hold_Days|0]|posture=full|objective_held=[?ic_afk_stage_objective|0]" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|day=[?FRE_Atlante_Days_Elapsed|0]|hold=[?FRE_Atlante_Hold_Days|0]|posture=vietnamese_led|objective_held=[?ic_afk_stage_objective|0]" }
		}
	}
	else = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_atlante_stage_last = 0 } }

	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Preserved has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Superseded } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Terminal_Reason } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Pollux_Terminal_Reason
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Preserved }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Preserved_Escort } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=preserved|reason=corridor_held_escort" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=preserved|reason=camp_held_airlift" }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss_Origin } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=lai_chau_lost_before_launch" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss_Unfunded } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=unfunded" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss_Escort } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=corridor_not_held_escort" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss_Airlift } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=camp_not_held_airlift" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Result_Column_Loss_Historical } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=historical_split" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=column_loss|reason=unclassified" }
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=war_over" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=geneva_concluded" }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase > 8 } } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=phase_closed" }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VIN } } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=vin_missing" }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = TAI } } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=tai_missing" }
			else_if = { limit = { VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active } } log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=dien_bien_phu_campaign_launched" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|POLLUX_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=camp_lost_or_not_established" }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { fre_atlante_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Terminal_Reason } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Atlante_Terminal_Reason
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Central_Success }
			log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=central_success|reason=full_objectives_held"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Vietnamese_Foothold }
			log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=vietnamese_foothold|reason=northern_objective_held"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Northern_Priority }
			log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=northern_priority|reason=northern_priority_chosen"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Atlante_Result_Stalled }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Result_Stalled_Full } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=stalled|reason=deadline_hold_short_full" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Result_Stalled_Vietnamese } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=stalled|reason=deadline_hold_short_vietnamese_led" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=stalled|reason=unfunded" }
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=war_over" }
			else_if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=geneva_concluded" }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase > 8 } } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=phase_closed" }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VIE } } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=vie_missing" }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = NLF } } log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=nlf_missing" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|ATLANTE_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=superseded|reason=vie_nlf_war_ended" }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Force_Hedgehog has_country_flag = FRE_Hedgehog_Network_Chosen NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Forced_Hedgehog_Check } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Forced_Hedgehog_Check
		log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Forced_hedgehog_chosen_by_AI"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Force_Hedgehog has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Castor_Launched NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Forced_Hedgehog_Check } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Forced_Hedgehog_Check
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Forced_hedgehog_chosen_by_AI|detail=castor_launched"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_garrison_removal_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_garrison_check_wait > 1 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.ic_afk_garrison_check_wait = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_garrison_check_wait = 1 } }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_garrison_check_wait = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = FRE_Preparation_Garrison_Deployed }
			log = "IC_AFK|INFO|date=[GetDateText]|check=Launch_garrison_removed_skipped|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_garrison_check_campaign|0]|reason=redeployed_for_campaign_[?global.ic_afk_garrison_campaign|0]"
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_garrison_left = 0 }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_template = "Groupe de Defense Locale" } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_garrison_left = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI TAI = { has_template = "Groupe de Defense Locale" } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_garrison_left = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_template = "Groupe de Defense Locale" } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_garrison_left = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_garrison_left = 0 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Launch_garrison_removed|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_garrison_check_campaign|0]|owners_with_template=0"
			}
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Launch_garrison_removed|campaign=[?global.ic_afk_garrison_check_campaign|0]|owners_with_template=[?ic_afk_garrison_left|0]" }
		}
	}
}

ic_afk_force_hedgehog_enable = {
	set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Force_Hedgehog
	log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEDGEHOG|date=[GetDateText]|state=enabled"
}

ic_afk_force_hedgehog_disable = {
	clr_global_flag = IC_AFK_Force_Hedgehog
	log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEDGEHOG|date=[GetDateText]|state=disabled"
}
