	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_p1287 = 0 }	# Atlante objective
	if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1287 size > 0 } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_p1287 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1287 size > 3 } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_p1287 = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1287 size > 7 } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_p1287 = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1287 size > 14 } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_p1287 = 4 } }
	log = "IC_AFK|PLACEMENT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=[THIS.GetTag]|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]|s1760=[?ic_afk_p1760|0]|s786=[?ic_afk_p786|0]|s881=[?ic_afk_p881|0]|s1280=[?ic_afk_p1280|0]|s1768=[?ic_afk_p1768|0]|s1281=[?ic_afk_p1281|0]|s1761=[?ic_afk_p1761|0]|s671=[?ic_afk_p671|0]|s1766=[?ic_afk_p1766|0]|s1762=[?ic_afk_p1762|0]|s1763=[?ic_afk_p1763|0]|s838=[?ic_afk_p838|0]|s1767=[?ic_afk_p1767|0]|s1287=[?ic_afk_p1287|0]"
}

ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_at_war = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_at_war = 1 } }
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_over_cap = 0 }
	if = { limit = { CWIC_has_exceeded_maximum_divisions = yes } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_over_cap = 1 } }
	log = "IC_AFK|ARMY_CAP|date=[GetDateText]|tag=[THIS.GetTag]|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]|cap=[?CWIC_max_divisions|0]|counted=[?CWIC_num_actual_divisions|0]|over_cap=[?ic_afk_over_cap|0]|war=[?ic_afk_at_war|0]|manpower_k=[?manpower_k|0]|gdp=[?grossdomesticproduct|0]"
}

ic_afk_placement_context = {
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_camp = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_camp = VIN.VIN_camp_current }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_op = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_op = FRE.FRE_op_current }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_signature = ic_afk_ctx_camp }
	multiply_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_signature = 100 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_signature = ic_afk_ctx_op }
}

ic_afk_placement_sweep = {
	ic_afk_placement_context = yes
	log = "IC_AFK|PLACEMENT_CTX|date=[GetDateText]|reason=[?ic_afk_ctx_reason|0]|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]|vin_campaign=[?ic_afk_ctx_camp|0]|fre_operation=[?ic_afk_ctx_op|0]"
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO } THO = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = MEO } MEO = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { ic_afk_placement_snapshot = yes } }
}

ic_afk_army_cap_sweep = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = LOS } LOS = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = LAO } LAO = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = MEO } MEO = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO } THO = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { ic_afk_army_cap_snapshot = yes } }
}

# Periodic sampling plus an extra sweep on every campaign/operation transition,
# which is where misdirected front effort shows up. Reason codes: 1 periodic,
# 2 campaign/operation change, 3 theatre closed.
ic_afk_force_observer_tick = {
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_days = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_days > 29 } }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_days = 0 }
		ic_afk_army_cap_sweep = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } }
			set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_reason = 1 }
			ic_afk_placement_sweep = yes
		}
	}
	ic_afk_placement_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			NOT = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_signature = ic_afk_ctx_signature } }
		}
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_force_signature = ic_afk_ctx_signature }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_reason = 2 }
		ic_afk_placement_sweep = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Force_Final } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Force_Final
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ctx_reason = 3 }
		ic_afk_placement_sweep = yes
		ic_afk_army_cap_sweep = yes
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_fre_highlands_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Mang_Yang_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Mang_Yang_Launch
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Posture_Road } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|choice=road|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Posture_Airlift } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|choice=airlift|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Posture_Hold } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|choice=hold|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mang_Yang_posture_selected" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Destroyed
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Mauled
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Escaped
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Airlifted
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Held
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Superseded
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Mang_Yang_Result }
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Mang_Yang_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Destroyed } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=destroyed" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Mauled } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=mauled" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Escaped } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=escaped" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Airlifted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=airlifted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Held } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=held" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Mang_Yang|outcome=superseded" }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Active has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Choice_Pending } }
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mang_Yang_active_cleanup"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mang_Yang_active_cleanup" }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Mang_Yang_Result_Destroyed }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_GM100_Deployed } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mang_Yang_unit_cleanup" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Mang_Yang_unit_cleanup" }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chu_Dreh_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chu_Dreh_Launch
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Posture_Road } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|choice=road|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" }
		else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Posture_Hold } log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|choice=hold|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chu_Dreh_posture_selected" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Mauled
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Escaped
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Held
				has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Superseded
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chu_Dreh_Result }
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chu_Dreh_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Mauled } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|outcome=mauled" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Escaped } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|outcome=escaped" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Held } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|outcome=held" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|package=Chu_Dreh|outcome=superseded" }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Active has_country_flag = FRE_Highlands_Chu_Dreh_Choice_Pending } }
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chu_Dreh_active_cleanup"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chu_Dreh_active_cleanup" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_dak_doa_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Dak_Doa_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Dak_Doa_Launch
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|hold=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days|0]/7|deadline_clean=21|deadline_final=45"
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_marker_active|province=1328" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_marker_active|province=1328" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { VIN_dak_doa_campaign_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Dak_Doa_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Dak_Doa_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|outcome=clean|day=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|outcome=costly|day=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|outcome=aborted|day=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|outcome=failure|day=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=NLF|campaign=Dak_Doa|outcome=superseded|day=[?NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Owns_FUL_War } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_owned_war_cleaned" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_owned_war_cleaned" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_marker_removed" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Dak_Doa_marker_removed" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_dak_doa_gate_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1954.02.10
			country_exists = VIN
			VIN = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = VIN_Dak_Doa_Offensive } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Dak_Doa_Launch }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_dak_doa_gate_days = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_dak_doa_gate_days > 29 } }
			set_variable = { global.ic_afk_dak_doa_gate_days = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_prereq = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_nlf = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_ful = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_fre = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_wider = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_10180 = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { g_ready = 0 }
			VIN = {
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = VIN_Promote_Le_Duc_Tho_Orthodoxy has_completed_focus = VIN_Retain_Nguyen_Binh_United_Front } } set_temp_variable = { g_prereq = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { VIN_dak_doa_available_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { g_ready = 1 } }
			}
			if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } set_temp_variable = { g_nlf = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FUL } set_temp_variable = { g_ful = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } set_temp_variable = { g_fre = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = NLF }
				NLF = {
					if = { limit = { NLF_dak_doa_wider_southern_war_trigger = yes } set_temp_variable = { g_wider = 1 } }
					if = { limit = { controls_province = 10180 } set_temp_variable = { g_10180 = 1 } }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|DAK_DOA_GATE|date=[GetDateText]|prereq_focus=[?g_prereq|0]|available=[?g_ready|0]|nlf=[?g_nlf|0]|ful=[?g_ful|0]|fre=[?g_fre|0]|nlf_wider_war=[?g_wider|0]|nlf_holds_10180=[?g_10180|0]|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]"
		}
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_daily_tick = {
	ic_afk_validation_initialize = yes
	ic_afk_validation_score_delta_tick = yes
	ic_afk_validation_dak_doa_gate_tick = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.ic_afk_heartbeat_days = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.ic_afk_heartbeat_days > 179 } }
		set_variable = { global.ic_afk_heartbeat_days = 0 }
		log = "IC_AFK|HEARTBEAT|date=[GetDateText]|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]|ending=[?global.indochina_ending_available_check|0]"
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_score_margin = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_score_margin = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore }
		log = "IC_AFK|STRUGGLE_SCORE|date=[GetDateText]|communist=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore|0]|pro_france=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore|0]|pro_independence=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore|0]|pro_ethnic=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore|0]|anti_total=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore|0]|communist_margin=[?ic_afk_score_margin|0]"
		log = "IC_AFK|GENEVA_LEVERAGE|date=[GetDateText]|communist=[?global.geneva_lev_communist|0]|pro_france=[?global.geneva_lev_profrance|0]|pro_independence=[?global.geneva_lev_proindependence|0]|federal=[?global.geneva_lev_federal|0]|ethnic=[?global.geneva_lev_ethnic|0]|usa=[?global.geneva_lev_usa|0]"
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { log = "IC_AFK|GENEVA_WEIGHT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIN|weight=[?geneva_weight|0]" } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { log = "IC_AFK|GENEVA_WEIGHT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIE|weight=[?geneva_weight|0]" } }
		log = "IC_AFK|GENEVA_WEIGHT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=FRA|weight=[?geneva_weight|0]"
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIN|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=FRE|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { log = "IC_AFK|METROPOLE|date=[GetDateText]|patience=[?FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]|war_credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]" } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=TAI|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=TAI|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=TAM|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=TAM|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=NUN|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=NUN|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO } THO = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=THO|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=THO|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIE|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIE|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=NLF|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=NLF|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = LOS } LOS = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=LOS|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=LOS|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = LAO } LAO = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=LAO|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=LAO|status=collapsed" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = MEO } MEO = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=MEO|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FORCE_HEALTH|date=[GetDateText]|tag=MEO|status=collapsed" }
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_validation_vin_launch_tick = yes ic_afk_validation_vin_result_tick = yes ic_afk_validation_vin_cleanup_tick = yes ic_afk_validation_vin_post_dbp_strategy_tick = yes ic_afk_validation_vin_surrender_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_validation_lower_laos_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_validation_command_inputs_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { ic_afk_validation_patronage_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { ic_afk_validation_dak_doa_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_fre_limited_tick = yes ic_afk_validation_fre_late_packages_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_fre_new_operations_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_fre_stage_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_fre_highlands_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_associated_armies_tick = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { ic_afk_validation_chanson_tick = yes } }
	ic_afk_validation_garrison_removal_tick = yes
	ic_afk_validation_laos_raid_tick = yes
	ic_afk_validation_luang_prabang_tick = yes
	ic_afk_force_observer_tick = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_NUN_Postwar_Status } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_NUN_Postwar_Status
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = NUN }
			NUN = {
				if = { limit = { has_capitulated = yes } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR|date=[GetDateText]|exists=yes|capitulated=yes|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" }
				else = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR|date=[GetDateText]|exists=yes|capitulated=no|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" }
			}
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR|date=[GetDateText]|exists=no|capitulated=not_applicable" }
		if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = NUN } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=NUN" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = VIE } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=VIE" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=VIN" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = FRA } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=FRA" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = FRE } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=FRE" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=other" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_NUN_Postwar_Terminal }
			OR = {
				NOT = { country_exists = NUN }
				NUN = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_NUN_Postwar_Terminal
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|exists=yes|capitulated=yes|divisions=[?num_divisions|0]" } }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL|date=[GetDateText]|exists=no|capitulated=not_applicable" }
		if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = NUN } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=NUN" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = VIE } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=VIE" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=VIN" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = FRA } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=FRA" }
		else_if = { limit = { 1281 = { is_owned_by = FRE } } log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=FRE" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|NUN_POSTWAR_TERMINAL_OWNERSHIP|date=[GetDateText]|state=1281|owner=other" }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_completed_focus = FRE_Preparing_for_the_Final_Push } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Final_Push } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Final_Push
		if = { limit = { FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Final_Push_Preparation } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Final_Push_consumed_terminal_Mouette_and_Atlante_results" }
		else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase > 8 } NOT = { country_exists = VIN } } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Final_Push_theatre_closure_bypass" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Final_Push_completion_contract" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Geneva_Conclusion } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Geneva_Conclusion
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_score_margin = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { ic_afk_score_margin = StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore }
		log = "IC_AFK|FINAL_BALANCE|date=[GetDateText]|communist=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore|0]|anti_total=[?StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore|0]|communist_margin=[?ic_afk_score_margin|0]|communist_leverage=[?global.geneva_lev_communist|0]|fra_weight=[?geneva_weight|0]"
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = { log = "IC_AFK|FINAL_GENEVA_WEIGHT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIN|weight=[?geneva_weight|0]" } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { log = "IC_AFK|FINAL_GENEVA_WEIGHT|date=[GetDateText]|tag=VIE|weight=[?geneva_weight|0]" } }
		log = "IC_AFK|END|date=[GetDateText]|result=scripted_Geneva_concluded|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Theatre_End } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Theatre_End
		log = "IC_AFK|END|date=[GetDateText]|result=Indochina_War_Over|phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0]"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_command_inputs_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Command_Inputs_Applied VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Command_Inputs_Leak } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Command_Inputs_Leak
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Command_inputs_flag_cleared_at_cleanup|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Command_Inputs_Applied NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Command_Inputs_Pass } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Command_Inputs_Pass
		log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Command_inputs_applied_once_per_launch|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|supply_total=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_peace_conference_start = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { ROOT = { ic_failsafe_theatre_tag_trigger = yes } FROM = { ic_failsafe_theatre_tag_trigger = yes } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded }
		}
		log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=no_vanilla_Indochina_peace_conference|winner=[ROOT.GetTag]|loser=[FROM.GetTag]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { ROOT = { ic_failsafe_theatre_tag_trigger = yes } FROM = { ic_failsafe_theatre_tag_trigger = yes } } }
		log = "IC_AFK|INFO|date=[GetDateText]|check=later_theatre_peace_conference_after_scripted_end|winner=[ROOT.GetTag]|loser=[FROM.GetTag]"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_lower_laos_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Lower_Laos_Launch } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Lower_Laos_Launch
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ll_atlante = 0 }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active } } set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_ll_atlante = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Lower_Laos|days=45|atlante_active=[?ic_afk_ll_atlante|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_ll_atlante = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted } has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Atlante_Hold_Applied } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_atlante_pressure_applied|hold_extension=[?FRE.FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension|0]" }
			else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_atlante_pressure_applied" }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { vin_lower_laos_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Lower_Laos_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Lower_Laos_Result
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Lower_Laos|outcome=pressure|days=[?VIN_Lower_Laos_Days|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Lower_Laos|outcome=contained|days=[?VIN_Lower_Laos_Days|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Lower_Laos|outcome=superseded|days=[?VIN_Lower_Laos_Days|0]|supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_package_cleared" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_package_cleared" }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted } }
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_idea_removed"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_idea_removed" }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { country_exists = LOS has_war_with = LOS }
					AND = { country_exists = CAM has_war_with = CAM }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_no_war_with_LOS_or_CAM"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Lower_Laos_no_war_with_LOS_or_CAM" }
	}
}

		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Camargue_garrison_deployed_or_VIE_absent|operation=7" }
		ic_afk_validation_fre_balance_contract = yes
	}

		limit = { FRE_bretagne_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_5_Result } }

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Bretagne_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=5|name=Bretagne|outcome=costly" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Bretagne_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=5|name=Bretagne|outcome=aborted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Bretagne_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=5|name=Bretagne|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Bretagne_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=5|name=Bretagne|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Bretagne_Activated } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=5" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=5" }

		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_6_Result

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Aborted } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=6|name=Adolphe|outcome=aborted" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=6|name=Adolphe|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=6|name=Adolphe|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Adolphe_Activated } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=6" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=6" }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Clean has_country_flag = FRE_Adolphe_Sortie_Success }
					AND = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Clean } NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Adolphe_Sortie_Success } }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Adolphe_sortie_flag_matches_result|operation=6"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Adolphe_sortie_flag_matches_result|operation=6" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { FRE_camargue_terminal_trigger = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_7_Result } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_FRE_7_Result
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_fre_new_result = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Camargue_Result_Clean } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=7|name=Camargue|outcome=clean" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Camargue_Result_Costly } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=7|name=Camargue|outcome=costly" }

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Camargue_Result_Failure } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=7|name=Camargue|outcome=failure" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Camargue_Result_Superseded } log = "IC_AFK|RESULT|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|operation=7|name=Camargue|outcome=superseded" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Camargue_Activated } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=7" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_operation_marker_removed|operation=7" }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Camargue_Garrison_Deployed } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Camargue_garrison_removed|operation=7" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Camargue_garrison_removed|operation=7" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active } OR = { FRE_bretagne_terminal_trigger = yes FRE_adolphe_terminal_trigger = yes FRE_camargue_terminal_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Light has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Standard has_idea = FRE_Limited_Operation_Maximum } NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_FRE_Commitment_Cleanup } } set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Failed_FRE_Commitment_Cleanup log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_commitment_cleanup" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_fre_new_result = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 0 }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN has_war_with = VIN } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI TAI = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM TAM = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN NUN = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO THO = { has_war_with = VIN } } add_to_temp_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { ic_afk_operation_wars = 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Owns_War } } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=0" }
		else_if = {
			# A superseding VIN campaign declares its own war in the same tick; those wars belong to it.
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Owns_War } country_exists = VIN VIN = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } } }
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=0|vin_campaign_wars=[?ic_afk_operation_wars|0]|vin_campaign=[?VIN.VIN_camp_current|0]"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=FRE_limited_operation_full_cleanup|remaining_operation_wars=[?ic_afk_operation_wars|0]" }
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_patronage_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Patronage_Check }
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Patronage_Check
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet }
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Patronage_exclusive|chinese=1|soviet=1"
		}
		else = {
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Patronage_exclusive"
		}
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_associated_armies_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = FRE_Arm_the_National_Army NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_VNA } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_VNA
		if = { limit = { country_exists = LOS } LOS = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_assoc_los = num_divisions } } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = CAM } CAM = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_assoc_cam = num_divisions } } }
		log = "IC_AFK|ASSOCIATED_ARMIES|date=[GetDateText]|choice=vna|los_divisions=[?global.ic_afk_assoc_los|0]|cam_divisions=[?global.ic_afk_assoc_cam|0]|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]|vie_cap=30"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Associated_Armies_Chosen NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_Check } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_Check
		if = { limit = { country_exists = LOS } LOS = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_assoc_los = num_divisions } } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = CAM } CAM = { set_variable = { global.ic_afk_assoc_cam = num_divisions } } }
		log = "IC_AFK|ASSOCIATED_ARMIES_STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|los_divisions=[?global.ic_afk_assoc_los|0]|cam_divisions=[?global.ic_afk_assoc_cam|0]"
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = LOS
				country_exists = CAM
				LOS = { has_template = "Armee Nationale Laotienne" has_country_flag = LOS_Associated_Army_Raised }
				CAM = { has_template = "Armee Nationale Khmere" has_country_flag = CAM_Associated_Army_Raised }
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Associated_armies_templates_loaded"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Associated_armies_templates_loaded" }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_country_flag = VIE_Associated_Armies_Cap_Reduced } }
			log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Associated_armies_vie_cap_flag"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Associated_armies_vie_cap_flag" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = FRE_Associated_Armies_Chosen
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_Lower_Laos }
			country_exists = VIN
			VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained }
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Associated_Armies_Lower_Laos
		log = "IC_AFK|ASSOCIATED_ARMIES_LOWER_LAOS|date=[GetDateText]|outcome=contained|source=royal_lao_army"
	}
}

ic_afk_validation_chanson_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Killed NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Killed } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Killed
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Survived } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_outcomes_exclusive" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_outcomes_exclusive" }
		if = { limit = { has_character = FRE_Charles_Chanson } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_retired_on_death" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_retired_on_death" }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Chanson_Ascendancy } } }
			log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_nlf_ascendancy_set"
		}
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_nlf_ascendancy_set" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Survived NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Survived } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Survived
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Killed } log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_outcomes_exclusive" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_outcomes_exclusive" }
		if = { limit = { has_character = FRE_Charles_Chanson } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_kept_on_survival" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_kept_on_survival" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Pacification_Done NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Pacification } }
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Pacification
		if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Chanson_Pacification_Program } log = "IC_AFK|PASS|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_pacification_idea_applied" }
		else = { log = "IC_AFK|FAIL|date=[GetDateText]|check=Chanson_pacification_idea_applied" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = FRE_Chanson_Survived
			has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active
			check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 7 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Camargue }
		}
		set_global_flag = IC_AFK_Seen_Chanson_Camargue
		log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|campaign=Camargue|source=Chanson|price_light=[?FRE_op_price_light|0]|price_standard=[?FRE_op_price_standard|0]|price_maximum=[?FRE_op_price_maximum|0]|hold_required=6"
	}
}

# French Indochina command QA
# Console use from any tag: e test_indochina_command_status
# Destructive early-setup repair: e test_indochina_command_repair

test_indochina_command_status = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = FRE } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - CEFEO does not exist" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { NOT = { is_faction_leader = yes } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - CEFEO is not faction leader" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_French_Indochina_Command_Established } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - establishment flag missing" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { NOT = { has_idea = French_Indochina_Faction } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - CEFEO command identity missing" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { NOT = { has_idea = State_of_Vietnam_Faction } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - State of Vietnam no-call identity missing" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { NOT = { has_idea = Viet_Minh_Faction } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Hanoi no-call identity missing" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { NOT = { has_idea = Viet_Minh_Faction } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - southern resistance no-call identity missing" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = VIE VIE = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - State of Vietnam is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = CCC CCC = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Cochinchina is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = FUL FUL = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Montagnard crown domain is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = TAI TAI = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Tai Federation is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = NUN NUN = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Nung territory is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = TAM TAM = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Muong Federation is outside French Indochina" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE country_exists = THO THO = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Tho territory is outside French Indochina" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI TAI = { NOT = { is_subject_of = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Tai Federation is not in CEFEO custody" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN NUN = { NOT = { is_subject_of = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Nung territory is not in CEFEO custody" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM TAM = { NOT = { is_subject_of = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Muong Federation is not in CEFEO custody" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO THO = { NOT = { is_subject_of = FRE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Tho territory is not in CEFEO custody" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = CCC CCC = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VIE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Cochinchina is not in Saigon custody" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FUL FUL = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VIE } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Montagnard crown domain is not in Saigon custody" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE country_exists = VIN VIE = { has_war_with = VIN } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - State of Vietnam and Hanoi are direct belligerents" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = CCC country_exists = VIN CCC = { has_war_with = VIN } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Cochinchina leaked into Hanoi's northern war" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FUL country_exists = VIN FUL = { has_war_with = VIN } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Montagnard command leaked into Hanoi's northern war" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN country_exists = VIN NUN = { has_war_with = VIN } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Nung territory leaked into Hanoi's campaign" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = VIN NLF = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VIN } } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - southern resistance is outside Hanoi's faction" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { is_subject_of = FRA } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - State of Vietnam is a direct French subject" }
	if = { limit = { vin_nlf_coordination_active_trigger = yes } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: Hanoi-southern resistance coordination channel is active" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE country_exists = NLF VIE = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: southern war is active and separate" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = FRE NLF = { has_war_with = FRE } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - northern campaign leaked into the southern resistance" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = TAI NLF = { has_war_with = TAI } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Tai campaign leaked into the southern resistance" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = TAM NLF = { has_war_with = TAM } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Hoa Binh campaign leaked into the southern resistance" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF country_exists = THO NLF = { has_war_with = THO } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Cao-Bac campaign leaked into the southern resistance" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active country_exists = NLF country_exists = LOS NLF = { has_war_with = LOS } } log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: FAIL - Pathet Lao raid leaked into the southern resistance" }
	log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: status check complete"
}

test_indochina_command_repair = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			clr_country_flag = FRE_French_Indochina_Command_Established
			fre_indochina_command_initialize = yes
		}
		log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: faction and command membership rebuilt; run the status check"
	}
	else = { log = "INDOCHINA COMMAND: repair refused - CEFEO does not exist" }
}

test_binh_status = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VIN } } log = "BINH: FAIL - Hanoi does not exist" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = NLF } } log = "BINH: southern resistance is gone; the recall chain is inert" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } log = "BINH|route=[?VIN.VIN_Binh_Route|0]|date=[GetDateText]" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Censured } } log = "BINH: Hanoi censured him" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Defended } } log = "BINH: Hanoi defended him" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Escort_Committed } } log = "BINH: escort committed" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN VIN = { has_country_flag = Binh_Outcome } } log = "BINH: outcome recorded" }
	if = { limit = { any_country = { has_country_flag = FRE_Binh_Corridor_Interdiction } } log = "BINH: the Cambodian corridor is interdicted" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = NLF_Binh_Killed } } log = "BINH: killed in the ambush" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { NOT = { NLF_southern_command_settled_trigger = yes } } } log = "BINH: FAIL - no southern command flag is set" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = Nguyen_Binh_Leader } } log = "BINH: command = Nguyen Binh" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = Le_Duan_Leader } } log = "BINH: command = Le Duan" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = Le_Duc_Tho_Leader } } log = "BINH: command = Le Duc Tho" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = Tran_quoc_Buu_Leader } } log = "BINH: command = Tran Quoc Buu" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = Kieu_Cong_Cung_Leader } } log = "BINH: command = Kieu Cong Cung" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_idea = NLF_vietminh_disarray } } log = "BINH: NLF holds vietminh_disarray" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_idea = NLF_post_binh_disorganization } } log = "BINH: NLF holds post_binh_disorganization" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_idea = NLF_fractured_command_structure } } log = "BINH: NLF holds fractured_command_structure" }

	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = NLF_Cung_Displaced } } log = "BINH: Cung displaced from the southern command" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { has_country_flag = NLF_Buu_Displaced } } log = "BINH: Buu displaced from the southern command" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_country_flag = Cung_Defection } } log = "BINH: Cung has rallied to Saigon" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_character = NLF_Kieu_Cong_Cung } } log = "BINH: Cung is on Saigon's roster" }
}

# ============================================================================
# Viet Bac Autonomous Region QA / testing effects
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Run from the console with:   e <effect_name>
# Every effect here expects VIN scope - `tag VIN` first.
#
# The Geneva and struggle test sections live in this file too.
#
# Quick reference:
#   e test_viet_bac_setup       hand VIN the four states and mark the war over
#   e test_viet_bac_ask         fire THO.1 now, skipping the 240-day queue
#   e test_viet_bac_establish   run the founding payload directly, no event
#   e test_viet_bac_check       assert the region came out correctly
#   e test_viet_bac_reset       tear it all down (rerunnable)
#
# A full pass is:
#   e test_viet_bac_setup / e test_viet_bac_ask / pick an option
#   e test_viet_bac_check
#   e test_viet_bac_reset
# ============================================================================

# `e test_viet_bac_setup` - the preconditions the on_daily_VIN gate looks for.
test_viet_bac_setup = {
	set_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO }
		annex_country = { target = THO transfer_troops = no }
	}
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_question_asked
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_granted
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_refused
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_established
	transfer_state = 1280
	transfer_state = 1768
	transfer_state = 881
	transfer_state = 1767
	log = "VIET BAC: setup done - VIN holds 1280, 1768, 881, 1767"
}

# `e test_viet_bac_ask` - skip the queue and put the decision on screen now.
test_viet_bac_ask = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_question_asked
	country_event = THO.1
}

# `e test_viet_bac_establish` - the founding payload with no event in the way,
# for checking the state and politics handover on its own.
test_viet_bac_establish = {
	vin_establish_viet_bac_zone = yes
	log = "VIET BAC: establishment effect run"
}

# `e test_viet_bac_check` - assertions. Silence is a pass.
test_viet_bac_check = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = THO } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - THO does not exist"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO NOT = { THO = { is_subject_of = VIN } } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - THO is not a subject of VIN"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { 1280 = { is_owned_by = THO } } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - Cao Bang is not THO's"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { 1768 = { is_owned_by = THO } } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - Lang Son is not THO's"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { 881 = { is_owned_by = THO } } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - Dong Bac Bo is not THO's"
	}
	if = {	# only a failure when Hanoi actually held Ha Giang to give
		limit = {
			NOT = { 1767 = { is_owned_by = THO } }
			NOT = { 1767 = { is_owned_by = MEO } }
		}
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - Ha Giang went to neither THO nor MEO"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { THO = { has_country_flag = THO_viet_bac_zone } } }
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - zone flag missing"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				THO = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = VIN_Chu_Van_Tan
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		log = "VIET BAC: FAIL - Chu Van Tan is not chairing the zone"
	}
	log = "VIET BAC: check complete"
}

# `e test_viet_bac_reset` - put the north back the way it was so the whole
# chain can be run again from a single save.
test_viet_bac_reset = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO }
		VIN_Chu_Van_Tan = { set_nationality = VIN }
		THO = { drop_cosmetic_tag = yes }
		annex_country = { target = THO transfer_troops = no }
	}
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_question_asked
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_granted
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_refused
	clr_country_flag = VIN_viet_bac_zone_established
	log = "VIET BAC: reset done"
}

test_tho_startup_check = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = THO } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - THO does not exist" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { 1280 = { is_owned_by = THO } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - Cao Bang is not THO-owned" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { 1768 = { is_owned_by = THO } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - Lang Son is not THO-owned" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { 881 = { is_owned_by = VIN } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - Dong Bac Bo is not VIN-owned" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { capital_scope = { state = 881 } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - VIN capital is not Dong Bac Bo" }
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			NOT = { THO = { is_subject_of = FRE } }
		}
		log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - THO is not a CEFEO subject"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "Together for Victory"
			THO = { NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_crown_domain } }
		}
		log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - THO is not a crown domain with Together for Victory"
	}
	if = { limit = { THO = { NOT = { has_cosmetic_tag = THO_FRA } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - colonial cosmetic tag missing" }
	if = { limit = { THO = { check_variable = { num_divisions < 2 } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - fewer than two defensive units" }
	if = { limit = { THO = { check_variable = { num_divisions > 2 } } } log = "THO STARTUP: FAIL - more than two starting units" }
	log = "THO STARTUP: check complete"
}
# VIN campaign lifecycle QA effects
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Console use: tag VIN, then `e <effect_name>`. Boundary effects classify the
# current map state without advancing a day; arrange objective control first.
# ============================================================================

test_vin_campaign_status = {
	log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: id [?VIN_camp_current|0], day [?VIN_camp_days_elapsed|0], clean [?VIN_camp_deadline_x|0], final [?VIN_camp_deadline_y|0], last outcome [?VIN_camp_last_outcome|0]"
	if = { limit = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: no active campaign" }
	else = { log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: active campaign found" }
	if = { limit = { vin_campaign_objective_met_trigger = yes } log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: objective met" }
	else = { log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: objective not met" }
	if = { limit = { vin_campaign_superseded_trigger = yes } log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: theatre/target supersession detected" }
	if = { limit = { vin_campaign_war_abandoned_trigger = yes } log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: absent-war abort detected" }
}

test_vin_campaign_overextension_status = {
	vin_campaign_update_overextension = yes
	if = {
		limit = { vin_campaign_vin_overextended_trigger = yes }
		log = "VIN OVEREXTENSION: off-envelope foreign ground detected, held [?VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days|0] days"
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe } check_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days > 4 } } log = "VIN OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - VIN penalty missing after the 5-day grace" }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension check_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days < 5 } NOT = { vin_fre_operation_overextended_trigger = yes } } log = "VIN OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - VIN penalty applied inside the 5-day grace" }
	}
	else = {
		log = "VIN OVEREXTENSION: VIN remains inside its operational envelope"
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } log = "VIN OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - VIN penalty did not clear" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { vin_campaign_french_overextended_trigger = yes }
		log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: a CEFEO-aligned tag controls VIN rear-area ground"
	}
	else = {
		log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: defensive side remains inside its envelope"
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending FRE command lacks penalty" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI TAI = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending TAI command lacks penalty" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM TAM = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending TAM command lacks penalty" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN NUN = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending NUN command lacks penalty" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO THO = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending THO command lacks penalty" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } } log = "FRENCH OVEREXTENSION: FAIL - offending VIE command lacks penalty" }
}

# Repairs old saves, reapplies the current operation's planned ground, and then
# reports the state-policy invariants which caused the 2026-08-09 playtest
# failure. Run once immediately after every campaign starts.
test_vin_campaign_state_policy_status = {
	vin_campaign_sync_objective_modifiers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes }
		log = "VIN STATE POLICY: no campaign; checking restored theatre baseline"
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = VIN_unplanned_offensive_flag
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } }
			}
			log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - a marked theatre state is missing unplanned_offensive"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { 1761 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Tay Bac objective 1761 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 671 = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Tay Bac exterior 671 is open" }
		log = "VIN STATE POLICY: Tay Bac planned=1761; protected exterior includes 671"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { 1761 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Hoa Binh approach 1761 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 1766 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Hoa Binh objective 1766 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 671 = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Hoa Binh exterior 671 is open" }
		log = "VIN STATE POLICY: Hoa Binh planned=1761/1766; protected exterior includes 671"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { 1761 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Dien Bien approach 1761 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 671 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Dien Bien objective 671 is still unplanned" }
		log = "VIN STATE POLICY: Dien Bien planned=1761/671"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { 1280 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Cao Bang 1280 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 1768 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Lang Son 1768 is still unplanned" }
		if = { limit = { 881 = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Dong Bac Bo 881 is open" }
		if = { limit = { 1761 = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - TAI state 1761 is open during Cao-Bac" }
		if = { limit = { 1766 = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Hoa Binh 1766 is open during Cao-Bac" }
		log = "VIN STATE POLICY: Cao-Bac planned=1280/1768; Dong Bac/TAI/Hoa Binh protected"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { 1762 = { is_controlled_by = VIN NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - VIN-controlled Thanh Hoa lacks dispersed supply" }
		if = { limit = { 1763 = { is_controlled_by = VIN NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - VIN-controlled Nghe-Tinh lacks dispersed supply" }
		if = { limit = { 838 = { is_controlled_by = VIN NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - VIN-controlled Quang Binh lacks dispersed supply" }
	}
	else = {
		if = { limit = { 1762 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Thanh Hoa supply leaked past campaign cleanup" }
		if = { limit = { 1763 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Nghe-Tinh supply leaked past campaign cleanup" }
		if = { limit = { 838 = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } } log = "VIN STATE POLICY: FAIL - Quang Binh supply leaked past campaign cleanup" }
	}
	log = "VIN STATE POLICY: check complete"
}

}

	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - VIN penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - FRE penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - TAI penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - TAM penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN  NUN = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - NUN penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - THO penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE  VIE = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - VIE penalty remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - FRE campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - TAI campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - TAM campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN  NUN = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - NUN campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: FAIL - THO campaign-command spirit remains" }
	log = "OVEREXTENSION CLEANUP: check complete"
}

}

# Run as VIN when the target accepted the armistice but a French tag stayed at war.
test_vin_campaign_armistice_cleanup = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending
	vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { country_exists = FRE  has_war_with = FRE }
				AND = { country_exists = FRA  has_war_with = FRA }
				AND = { country_exists = TAI  has_war_with = TAI }
				AND = { country_exists = TAM  has_war_with = TAM }
				AND = { country_exists = THO  has_war_with = THO }
				AND = { country_exists = VIE  has_war_with = VIE }
				AND = { country_exists = NUN  has_war_with = NUN }
			}
		}
		log = "VIN ARMISTICE: a French-aligned belligerent remains; the daily retry is armed"
	}
	else = { log = "VIN ARMISTICE: all campaign belligerents removed" }
}

test_vin_campaign_save_checkpoint = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Save_Load_Checkpoint
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_save_id = VIN_camp_current }
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_save_day = VIN_camp_days_elapsed }
	log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: checkpoint stored at id [?VIN_camp_save_id|0], day [?VIN_camp_save_day|0]; save and reload"
}

test_vin_campaign_save_verify = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Save_Load_Checkpoint } }
		log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: FAIL - save/load checkpoint flag missing"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = VIN_camp_save_id } } }
		log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: FAIL - campaign id changed across save/load"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed < VIN_camp_save_day } }
		log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: FAIL - elapsed day moved backward across save/load"
	}
	log = "VIN CAMPAIGN: save/load verification complete"
}

test_vin_campaign_cao_bac_check = {
	set_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Result_Clean } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Result_Costly } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Result_Aborted } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Result_Failure } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { vin_test_result_count = 1 } } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - expected exactly one result flag" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - active flag remains" }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Cao_Bac } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - cooldown missing" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - resolver lock remains" }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Mobilization } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - VIN campaign spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - VIN overextension remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - FRE overextension remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - FRE campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - THO overextension remains" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - THO campaign-command spirit remains" }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { country_exists = THO  has_war_with = THO }
				AND = { country_exists = FRE  has_war_with = FRE }
			}
		}
		log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - campaign war remains"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 3 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - THO survived a successful campaign" }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { owns_state = 1280 } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - VIN does not own Cao Bang" }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { owns_state = 1768 } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - VIN does not own Lang Son" }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = Enable_Air_Base_Raids } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - air-base raids were not unlocked" }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 3 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 4 }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { country_exists = THO } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - THO vanished after abort/failure" }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { 1280 = { is_owned_by = THO } } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - abort/failure changed Cao Bang ownership" }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { 1768 = { is_owned_by = THO } } } log = "VIN CAO-BAC: FAIL - abort/failure changed Lang Son ownership" }
	}
	log = "VIN CAO-BAC: result check complete"
}

# Post-DBP QA: status is read-only; focus_geneva_check advances the live checks;
# response_reset preserves the Dien Bien Phu recorder and current map state.
test_vin_post_dbp_southern_war_status = {
	log = "[VIN SOUTH] dbp=[?global.geneva_outcome_dbp|0] phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0] A=[?global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points|0] B=[?global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points|0]"
	set_temp_variable = { vin_post_dbp_strategy_choice_count = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Negotiations_Chosen } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_post_dbp_strategy_choice_count = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen } add_to_temp_variable = { vin_post_dbp_strategy_choice_count = 1 } }
	log = "[VIN SOUTH] strategic choice count=[?vin_post_dbp_strategy_choice_count|0] (expected 0 before choice, exactly 1 afterward)"
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_dbp_outcome_applied } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Dien Bien Phu outcome recorded" }
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] BLOCKED - Dien Bien Phu outcome not recorded" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_War_Recognized } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Continued southern war recognized" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Funding response chosen" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Pressed_For_Geneva } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva response chosen" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_War_Ended } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern response ended" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF_Defeated } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Funded resistance was defeated" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF_Ceasefire } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Funded resistance reached a ceasefire" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Focus-owned Geneva is queued behind the peace gate" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Launched } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Focus-owned Geneva invitation launched" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued } log = "[VIN SOUTH] FAIL - legacy autonomous Geneva queue remains" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Disarmed_NLF_Armistice } log = "[VIN SOUTH] A disarmed southern resistance received the automatic armistice" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva pursuit is active" }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance country exists"
		if = { limit = { NLF = { has_capitulated = yes } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance has capitulated" }
		else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance has not capitulated" }
		if = { limit = { NLF = { num_of_controlled_states > 0 } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance still controls at least one state" }
		else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance controls no states" }
		if = { limit = { NLF = { num_divisions > 0 } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance still fields divisions" }
		else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance fields no divisions" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Saigon remains at war with the southern resistance" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] CEFEO remains at war with the southern resistance" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA FRA = { has_war_with = NLF } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] France remains at war with the southern resistance" }
	}
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance country no longer exists" }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF NLF = { is_subject = no } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] NLF remains independent" }
	else_if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } log = "[VIN SOUTH] WARNING - NLF is no longer independent" }
	if = {
		limit = { vin_post_dbp_southern_war_active_trigger = yes }
		log = "[VIN SOUTH] The NLF is alive in a post-DBP war; the response category should be visible"
	}
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] No live post-DBP NLF war is currently detected" }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } log = "[VIN SOUTH] BLOCKED - Hanoi is still at war" }
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Hanoi is at peace" }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		if = { limit = { VIE = { has_war = yes } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] BLOCKED - Saigon is still at war" }
		else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Saigon is at peace" }
	}
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] Saigon no longer exists; its peace clause passes" }
	if = { limit = { geneva_conference_vietnam_at_peace_trigger = yes } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Vietnamese peace gate passes" }
	else = { log = "[VIN SOUTH] BLOCKED - Vietnamese peace gate fails" }
	if = { limit = { vin_post_dbp_southern_resistance_defeated_trigger = yes } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Southern resistance defeat predicate passes" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Panic_Geneva_Suppressed } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Panic Geneva is suppressed by Hanoi's escalation choice" }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_National_Reunification_Preparation } log = "[VIN SOUTH] 180-day reunification preparation spirit is active" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Preparation_Cleaned NOT = { has_idea = VIN_National_Reunification_Preparation } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Reunification preparation cleanup passed" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Invasion_Declared country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Direct VIN-VIE invasion declaration passed" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_General_Offensive_Terminal NOT = { has_global_flag = Vietnam_War } } log = "[VIN SOUTH] General offensive did not activate the 1960s Vietnam War system" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_General_Offensive_Terminal } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Declaration owner contract: VIE only; no explicit FRE/FRA call exists in the post-DBP effect" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva has been announced" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference_Preparations } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva preparations are active" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva is in session" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded } log = "[VIN SOUTH] Geneva has concluded" }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF
			country_exists = NLF
			NLF = { NOT = { has_idea = NLF_Northern_Supply_Line } }
			vin_post_dbp_southern_war_active_trigger = yes
		}
		log = "[VIN SOUTH] FAIL - funding chosen but the live NLF lacks Northern Supply Line"
	}
}

	else = { log = "[FRE DBP] response inactive" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Response_Choice_Pending } log = "[FRE DBP] response choice pending" }

	if = { limit = { fre_dbp_condor_corridor_open_trigger = yes } log = "[FRE DBP] Condor map corridor currently passes" }
	else = { log = "[FRE DBP] Condor map corridor currently fails" }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Condor_Objective_Achieved } log = "[FRE DBP] Condor seven-day objective achieved" }

	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active } log = "[FRE DBP] parent campaign is live" }
			else = { log = "[FRE DBP] parent campaign is not live" }
			if = { limit = { vin_dbp_valley_invested_trigger = yes } log = "[FRE DBP] Viet Minh investment prerequisite passes" }
			else = { log = "[FRE DBP] Viet Minh investment prerequisite fails" }
			if = { limit = { controls_province = 4529 } log = "[FRE DBP] WARNING - Viet Minh currently control the camp objective" }
		}
	}
}

test_indochina_set_phase_high_intensity = {
    set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 3 }
    indochina_struggle_update_phase_transitions = yes
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = THIS.id }
            }
        }
        indochina_struggle_starting_idea_phase_change = yes
    }
}

                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = THIS.id }
                is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = THIS.id }
            }
        }
        indochina_struggle_starting_idea_phase_change = yes
    }
}
