test_indochina_manual_escalate = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 8 }
        }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 0 }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 0 }
        indochina_struggle_update_phase_transitions = yes
        every_country = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = THIS.id }
                }
            }
            indochina_struggle_starting_idea_phase_change = yes
            set_variable = { global.indochina_phase_check = 1 }
        }
    }
    else = {
    }
}

test_indochina_manual_deescalate = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 8 }
        }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 0 }
        set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 0 }
        indochina_struggle_update_phase_transitions = yes
        every_country = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = THIS.id }
                    is_in_array = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = THIS.id }
                }
            }
            indochina_struggle_starting_idea_phase_change = yes
            set_variable = { global.indochina_phase_check = 1 }
        }
    }
    else = {
    }
}

test_indochina_unlock_geneva = {
    test_indochina_set_phase_low_tension = yes
    set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 500 }
    if = {
        limit = {
            geneva_conference_should_be_available_trigger = yes
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 8 } } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points < 499 } }
        }
    }
}

            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        set_global_flag = Never_Ending_Conflict_Available
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 9 } }
                date < 1957.1.2
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference }
        }
    }
}

                    check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore > 499 }
                    check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore > 499 }
                    check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore > 499 }
                    check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore > 499 }
                }
            }

        }
    }
}

test_indochina_unlock_dan_quoc_peace = {
    test_indochina_reset = yes
    set_global_flag = dan_quoc_peace
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 1000 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 600 }
    if = {
        limit = {
            indochina_struggle_ending_dan_quoc_peace_trigger = yes
            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = dan_quoc_peace } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                VIE = {
                    NOT = {
                        has_country_leader = {
                            character = VIE_Ngo_Dinh_Diem
                            ruling_only = yes
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                VIN = {
                    NOT = { VIN_ho_chi_minh_in_power = yes }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore > FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance } }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore < 500 }
            }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_unlock_american_north_vietnam = {
    test_indochina_reset = yes
    USA = {
        complete_national_focus = USA_50s_Reestablish_Deer_Team
    }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 2000 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 300 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 200 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 200 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 700 }
    if = {
        limit = {
            indochina_struggle_ending_american_north_vietnam_diplomatic_trigger = yes
            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                VIN = {
                    NOT = { VIN_ho_chi_minh_in_power = yes }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                USA = {
                    NOT = {
                        has_completed_focus = USA_50s_Reestablish_Deer_Team
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                VIN = {
                    has_war_with = USA
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                OR = {
                    AND = {
                        check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore > 499 }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore > 999 } }
                    }
                    AND = {
                        check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore > 999 }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore > 1999 } }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore < 500 }
            }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_full_american_north_vietnam_setup = {
    test_indochina_reset = yes
    test_indochina_set_phase_medium_intensity = yes
    
    USA = {
        complete_national_focus = USA_50s_Sponser_CuongDe_Coup
        complete_national_focus = USA_50s_Reestablish_Deer_Team
    }
    
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 2500 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 400 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 300 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 300 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 1000 }
    test_indochina_recalculate_anti_communist = yes
    
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = VIN
            VIN = { NOT = { VIN_ho_chi_minh_in_power = yes } }
        }
        VIN_set_president_ho_chi_minh = yes
    }
    
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = USA
            country_exists = VIN
            VIN = { has_war_with = USA }
        }
        VIN = { white_peace = USA }
    }
    
    USA = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = VIN
            modifier = medium_increase
        }
    }
    VIN = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = USA
            modifier = medium_increase
        }
    }
    
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = SOV
            country_exists = VIN
        }
        VIN = {
            add_opinion_modifier = {
                target = USA
                modifier = large_increase
            }
            SOV = {
                add_opinion_modifier = {
                    target = VIN
                    modifier = medium_decrease
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
}

test_indochina_trigger_reunification_chain = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = USA
            indochina_struggle_ending_american_north_vietnam_diplomatic_trigger = yes
            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
            NOT = { USA = { has_country_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Started } }
        }
        USA = {
            country_event = { id = USA_VIN_Reunification.1 days = 1 }
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { indochina_struggle_ending_american_north_vietnam_diplomatic_trigger = yes } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { USA = { has_country_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Started } }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_advance_reunification_chain = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = USA
            USA = { has_country_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Started }
            NOT = { has_global_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Complete }
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { USA = { has_country_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Started } } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Complete }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_verify_ending_available = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_global_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Complete
            indochina_struggle_ending_american_north_vietnam_diplomatic_trigger = yes
            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        set_variable = { show_indochina_end_gui = 1 }
        set_variable = { struggle_ending_display_value = 6 }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = USA_VIN_Reunification_Complete } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { indochina_struggle_ending_american_north_vietnam_diplomatic_trigger = yes } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_unlock_southern_victory = {
    test_indochina_reset = yes
    test_indochina_set_phase_high_intensity = yes
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 500 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 1500 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 600 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 500 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 2600 }
    VIE = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = VIN
            modifier = protest_action_strong
        }
    }
    VIN = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = VIE
            modifier = protest_action_strong
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            indochina_struggle_ending_southern_victory_trigger = yes
            NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                VIE = {
                    has_government = communism
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = {
                    OR = {
                        has_war_with = VIN
                        AND = {
                            has_opinion = {
                                target = VIN
                                value < -50
                            }
                            VIN = {
                                has_opinion = {
                                    target = VIE
                                    value < -50
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 3 }
                        check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 4 }
                        check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 6 }
                        check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 7 }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_debug_state = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore > 0 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_global_flag = Geneva_Conference }
    }
    else = {
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
    }
    else = {
    }
}

test_indochina_setup_southern_victory_full_chain = {
    test_indochina_reset = yes
    test_indochina_set_phase_high_intensity = yes
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 500 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 2000 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 700 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 600 }
    set_variable = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 3300 }
    VIE = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = VIN
            modifier = protest_action_strong
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { owns_state = 1761 } }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    country_exists = VIN
                    VIN = { owns_state = 1761 }
                }
                VIN = { transfer_state = 1761 }
            }
        }
    }
    VIN = {
        add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = VIE
            modifier = protest_action_strong
        }
    }
}

test_indochina_bypass_liberator_focus = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            country_exists = VIE
            has_focus_tree = VIE_50s_Bao_Dai
        }
        VIE = {
            complete_national_focus = VIE_BaoDai_Liberator_of_Vietnam
        }
    }
    else = {
    }
}

test_indochina_test_southern_victory_chain = {
    test_indochina_setup_southern_victory_full_chain = yes
    test_indochina_bypass_liberator_focus = yes
}

# ============================================
# ============================================
# FROM = country running command, ROOT = selected country/state/character

d_ic_trigger_ending = {
    FROM = {
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "communist" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_communist_victory = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "southern" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_southern_victory = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "federal" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_federal_vietnam = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "balkanized" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_balkanized_vietnam = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "dan_quoc" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_dan_quoc_peace = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "american_north" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_american_north_vietnam = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "kuomintang" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_kuomintang_victory = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "geneva" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_geneva = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "never_ending" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_never_ending = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "failed_state" } }
            test_indochina_trigger_failed_state = yes
        }
        else = {
        }
    }
}

        }

    }

d_ic_set_phase = {
    FROM = {
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 3 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_high_intensity = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 4 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_medium_intensity = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 5 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_low_intensity = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 6 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_high_tension = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 7 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_medium_tension = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 8 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_low_tension = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 9 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_never_ending = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = 10 } }
            test_indochina_set_phase_failed_state = yes
        }
        else = {
        }
    }
}

d_ic_trigger_reunification = {
    FROM = {
        test_indochina_trigger_reunification_chain = yes
    }
}

d_ic_verify_ending = {
    FROM = {
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { args.0 = "american_north" } }
            test_indochina_verify_ending_available = yes
        }
        else = {
        }
    }
}

# ============================================================================
# Indochina failsafe QA harness
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Console only. Run from any country scope with "e <name>". The break_* effects
# each reproduce one of the edge cases the layer exists to catch; the router is
# then either left to the on_actions or invoked directly with
# test_ic_failsafe_route_now. Read the results out of the game log.
#
# The reported bug is test_ic_failsafe_break_vin_to_fre.
# ============================================================================

test_ic_failsafe_status = {
	log = "IC FAILSAFE: phase=[?global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase|0] ending_available=[?global.indochina_ending_available_check|0]"
	log = "IC FAILSAFE SCORES: comm=[?global.ic_score_comm|0] profr=[?global.ic_score_profr|0] proind=[?global.ic_score_proind|0] proeth=[?global.ic_score_proeth|0] kmt=[?global.ic_score_kmt|0] anticomm=[?global.ic_score_anticomm|0]"
	log = "IC FAILSAFE STALLS: carrier=[?global.ic_stall_carrier_days|0] decided=[?global.ic_stall_decided_days|0] geneva=[?global.ic_stall_geneva_days|0] latch=[?global.ic_stall_latch_days|0] unclicked=[?global.ic_stall_unclicked_days|0] nowar=[?global.ic_stall_nowar_days|0]"
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ic_failsafe_theatre_present } log = "IC FAILSAFE: armed" }
	else = { log = "IC FAILSAFE: NOT armed - ic_failsafe_theatre_present is unset" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } log = "IC FAILSAFE: Indochina_War_Over is SET" }
	else = { log = "IC FAILSAFE: Indochina_War_Over is unset" }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ic_failsafe_fired } log = "IC FAILSAFE: the router has run at least once" }
	if = { limit = { ic_failsafe_militarily_decided_trigger = yes } log = "IC FAILSAFE: militarily decided = TRUE" }
	if = { limit = { ic_failsafe_geneva_latched_dead_trigger = yes } log = "IC FAILSAFE: Geneva latch is DEAD" }
	if = { limit = { ic_failsafe_geneva_live_trigger = yes } log = "IC FAILSAFE: a conference is genuinely running" }
	if = { limit = { ic_failsafe_no_theatre_war_trigger = yes } log = "IC FAILSAFE: nobody in the theatre is at war" }
	if = { limit = { ic_failsafe_ending_owner_is_human_trigger = yes } log = "IC FAILSAFE: a human can take the ending (90 day grace)" }
	else = { log = "IC FAILSAFE: no human can take the ending (60 day grace)" }
}

test_ic_failsafe_route_now = {
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: forcing a route"
	indochina_failsafe_route_decisive_result = yes
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

test_ic_failsafe_preflight = {
	indochina_failsafe_preflight = yes
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: preflight complete"
}

test_ic_failsafe_postflight = {
	indochina_failsafe_postflight = yes
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: postflight complete"
}

test_ic_failsafe_arm = {
	set_global_flag = ic_failsafe_theatre_present
	set_global_flag = indochina_struggle_initialized
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: layer armed by hand"
}

# The reported bug. The corps takes Tonkin outright and the Viet Minh is wiped
# off the map without VIE ever having been the belligerent, which is exactly the
# case the old VIE_on_actions hook could not see.
test_ic_failsafe_break_vin_to_fre = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			transfer_state = 1760
			transfer_state = 786
			transfer_state = 838
			transfer_state = 881
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = VIN }
				annex_country = { target = VIN transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: FRE has taken Tonkin and annexed VIN"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

# The communist mirror the hooks never had: the State of Vietnam goes under and
# nothing in the old system notices.
test_ic_failsafe_break_vie_to_vin = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			transfer_state = 286
			transfer_state = 757
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = VIE }
				annex_country = { target = VIE transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: VIN has taken Saigon and annexed VIE"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

# France dies in Europe: on_daily_FRA stops and every FRA-scope score read goes
# with it. The carrier chain should pick the tick up on VIN.
test_ic_failsafe_kill_fra = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SOV }
			SOV = { annex_country = { target = FRA transfer_troops = no } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ENG }
			ENG = { annex_country = { target = FRA transfer_troops = no } }
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: France annexed - the watchdog should route in 14 days"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

test_ic_failsafe_kill_all_principals = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = PRC }
		PRC = {
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } annex_country = { target = VIN transfer_troops = no } }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } annex_country = { target = VIE transfer_troops = no } }
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: both Vietnams annexed by a power outside the theatre"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

# The half of the reported bug that left two French-aligned Vietnams.
test_ic_failsafe_puppet_vin_to_fra = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRA
			country_exists = VIN
		}
		FRA = {
			puppet = { target = VIN }
			set_autonomy = {
				target = VIN
				autonomy_state = autonomy_indon_union
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: VIN is now a French puppet - break_bad_subjects should free it"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

# The other half: a peace conference hands the Tai Federation ground it has no
# claim to. enforce_minority_homelands should strip it back to 671 and 1761.
test_ic_failsafe_bloat_tai = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI }
		TAI = {
			transfer_state = 786
			transfer_state = 881
			transfer_state = 1280
			transfer_state = 1762
			transfer_state = 1763
		}
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: TAI inflated with five states it must not own"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

# The one-way latch with nothing behind it: every non-Geneva ending is now shut
# and the conference will never convene.
test_ic_failsafe_geneva_latch = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	set_global_flag = Geneva_Conference
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session
	clr_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded
	clr_global_flag = Geneva_Conference_Negotiations_Complete
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_latch_days = 0 }
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: dead Geneva latch set - abort at 60 days, route at 90"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

test_ic_failsafe_geneva_stall = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	set_global_flag = Geneva_Conference
	set_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_geneva_days = 118 }
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: conference stalled - compile at 120, cut short at 150"
}

# Puts every counter two days short of its first threshold so the watchdog can
# be watched firing without waiting out the grace periods.
test_ic_failsafe_stall_arm = {
	test_ic_failsafe_arm = yes
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_carrier_days = 12 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_decided_days = 28 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_geneva_days = 118 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_latch_days = 58 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_unclicked_days = 58 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_nowar_days = 88 }
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: every stall counter armed two days short"
}

test_ic_failsafe_save_repair = {
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_save_repair_done
	indochina_failsafe_save_repair = yes
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: save repair re-run"
	test_ic_failsafe_status = yes
}

test_ic_failsafe_reset = {
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_routing
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_fired
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_geneva_aborted
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_warning_sent
	clr_global_flag = ic_failsafe_save_repair_done
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_carrier_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_decided_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_geneva_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_latch_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_unclicked_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.ic_stall_nowar_days = 0 }
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ic_failsafe_pc_handled }
		clr_country_flag = ic_failsafe_pc_handled
	}
	log = "IC FAILSAFE TEST: failsafe state reset"
}

# Read-only response-package contract validator. Callers set
# temp.response_validation_package before invoking it:
# 1 Lorraine, 2 Hoa Binh, 3 Dien Bien, 4 Castor, 5 Pollux, 6 Atlante, 7 Dak Doa.

	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_postures = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_parent = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_owned_war = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_temp = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { response_validation_callback = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { response_validation_package = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Deep } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_postures = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Limited } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_postures = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Withheld } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_postures = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Success } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Limited } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Failure } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Withheld } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { VIN = { OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Clean has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Costly has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Aborted has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Failure has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Superseded } } } set_temp_variable = { response_validation_parent = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { response_validation_package = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Hoa_Binh_Result_Superseded } add_to_temp_variable = { response_validation_results = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { VIN = { OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Result_Clean has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Result_Costly has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Result_Aborted has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Result_Failure has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Result_Superseded } } } set_temp_variable = { response_validation_parent = 1 } }

}

####################################################
# VIE border settlement QA effects - run as `e <name>` from
# the console, after `tag VIE`.
#
#   -- setup / teardown --
#   e test_vie_border_reset             tear down all ladder state (rerunnable)
#   e test_vie_border_setup_won         southern-victory shape; frees CAM/LOS
#   e test_vie_border_setup_geneva_20   favourable-Geneva shape instead
#   e test_vie_border_setup_pathet_lao  clause 5 = 2: 841 + 1295 to a VIN-puppet LAO
#   e test_vie_border_open              open the category without the focus
#   e test_vie_border_claim_tranninh    grant the Tran-Ninh claim + choice flag
#   -- driving the ladder --
#   e test_vie_border_demand_svay       fire the demand for 1799
#   e test_vie_border_demand_tranninh   ... 1794
#   e test_vie_border_demand_houaphanh  ... 1295
#   e test_vie_border_demand_phongsaly  ... 841
#   e test_vie_border_lapse             force the unanswered-demand failsafe (.4)
#   e test_vie_border_strand            park 1295 at stage 1 with no demand pending
#                                       (the daily cleanup must free it next tick)
#   e test_vie_border_max_pressure      stage 2 + pressure 100 on all four
#   e test_vie_border_clash_svay        run the clash against 1799
#   e test_vie_border_win               force the clash-won event
#   e test_vie_border_lose              force the clash-lost event
#   -- inspection / regression --
#   e test_vie_border_debug             stage + pressure + owner for all four
#   e test_brevet_refuse                fire Brevert_Lines.3 (scope-bug regression)
####################################################

test_vie_border_reset = {
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Settlement_Open
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Clash_Active
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Claimed_TranNinh_Chosen
	clr_global_flag = VIE_50s_Border_Settlement_Resolved
	set_variable = { VIE_border_demand_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { VIE_border_clash_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { VIE_border_attacker_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { VIE_brevet_attacker_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { VIE_brevet_defender_state = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Aggressor } remove_ideas = VIE_Regional_Aggressor }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_Regional_Hegemon } remove_ideas = VIE_Regional_Hegemon }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIE_Chastened_Frontier } remove_ideas = VIE_Chastened_Frontier }
	every_state = {	# state-scoped progress survives owner changes, so it must be zeroed explicitly
		limit = {
			OR = {
				state = 1799
				state = 1794
				state = 1295
				state = 841
			}
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 0 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_pressure = 0 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_clash_failed = 0 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_cb_taken = 0 }
	}
	clear_array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states
}

test_vie_border_setup_won = {
	set_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		annex_country = { target = VIN transfer_troops = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		overlord = { end_puppet = ROOT }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = CAM
				original_tag = LOS
			}
			is_subject_of = FRA
		}
		FRA = { end_puppet = PREV }
	}
}

test_vie_border_setup_geneva_20 = {
	set_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
	set_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded
	set_variable = { global.geneva_partition_line = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		overlord = { end_puppet = ROOT }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = CAM
				original_tag = LOS
			}
			is_subject_of = FRA
		}
		FRA = { end_puppet = PREV }
	}
}

test_vie_border_setup_pathet_lao = {
	set_variable = { global.geneva_laos_cambodia_term = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = LAO } }
		LOS = { release = LAO }
	}
	LAO = {
		transfer_state = 841
		transfer_state = 1295
		keep_current_politics = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = LAO
				autonomy_state = autonomy_easternbloc
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
	}
}

test_vie_border_open = {
	clear_array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states
	1799 = { add_to_array = { array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states value = THIS } }	# must mirror VIE_Indochina_Border_Settlement - state scopes, not bare numbers
	1794 = { add_to_array = { array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states value = THIS } }
	1295 = { add_to_array = { array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states value = THIS } }
	841 = { add_to_array = { array = global.VIE_border_dispute_states value = THIS } }
	set_country_flag = VIE_Border_Settlement_Open	# 1799 is claimed from game start, nothing to add
}

test_vie_border_claim_tranninh = {
	set_country_flag = VIE_Claimed_TranNinh_Chosen
	1295 = { add_claim_by = VIE }
	1794 = { add_claim_by = VIE }
}

test_vie_border_demand_svay = {
	1799 = { test_vie_border_demand_state = yes }
}

test_vie_border_demand_tranninh = {
	1794 = { test_vie_border_demand_state = yes }
}

test_vie_border_demand_houaphanh = {
	1295 = { test_vie_border_demand_state = yes }
}

test_vie_border_demand_phongsaly = {
	841 = { test_vie_border_demand_state = yes }
}

test_vie_border_demand_state = {	# state scope - mirrors VIE_Border_Table_Demand, where FROM is the state and ROOT the country
	ROOT = {
		clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending
		set_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending
	}
	set_variable = { ROOT.VIE_border_demand_state = THIS.id }
	set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 1 }
	save_global_event_target_as = VIE_border_demand_target
	owner = {
		save_global_event_target_as = VIE_border_demand_owner
		country_event = VIE_Border_Settlement.1
	}
}

test_vie_border_lapse = {	# run straight after a demand: skips the 32-day wait
	country_event = VIE_Border_Settlement.4
}

test_vie_border_strand = {	# the shape the old shared-variable bug left behind
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending
	1295 = { set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 1 } }
}

test_vie_border_max_pressure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				state = 1799
				state = 1794
				state = 1295
				state = 841
			}
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_border_stage = 2 }
		set_variable = { VIE_border_pressure = 100 }
	}
	clr_country_flag = VIE_Border_Demand_Pending
}

test_vie_border_clash_svay = {
	1799 = {	# VIE_border_start_clash raises the flag itself once a staging state is found
		save_global_event_target_as = VIE_border_clash_target
		owner = { save_global_event_target_as = VIE_border_clash_owner }
		set_variable = { ROOT.VIE_border_clash_state = THIS.id }
	}
	VIE_border_start_clash = yes
}

test_vie_border_win = {
	country_event = VIE_Border_Settlement.30
}

test_vie_border_lose = {
	country_event = VIE_Border_Settlement.31
}

test_vie_border_debug = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				state = 1799
				state = 1794
				state = 1295
				state = 841
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: border state [This.GetName] stage [?THIS.VIE_border_stage] pressure [?THIS.VIE_border_pressure] clash_failed [?THIS.VIE_border_clash_failed] cb_taken [?THIS.VIE_border_cb_taken]"
		if = {	# a province we already occupy resolves through .35, not a border war - the engine rejects a pair under one controller
			limit = { is_controlled_by = VIE }
			log = "[GetDateText]: border state [This.GetName] is already VIE-controlled - clash resolves as fait accompli"
		}
	}
	log = "[GetDateText]: VIE demand_state [?ROOT.VIE_border_demand_state] clash_state [?ROOT.VIE_border_clash_state] attacker_state [?ROOT.VIE_border_attacker_state]"	# these read as state_id minus 2^30, not as state numbers
}

test_brevet_refuse = {
	country_event = Brevert_Lines.3
}
